* Changed: in the footer, the Language switcher sits left of Kadence Pro's dark mode switch. Its kadence_footer_dark_mode callback runs at priority 5, before Kadence Pro's own (10), so it is first in markup and keyboard order; the header and mobile menu keep it right of the switch (priority 20). Same change as the Property Services Directory theme (wpallstars/propertyservicesdirectory GH#137).

= 1.3.142 =
* Fix: Load plugins only where needed also keeps what it learned when a plugin with a long identifier queues background work. The WP Background Processing library cuts its {identifier}_batch_{md5} keys to 64 characters, which can leave fewer than eight of the MD5's digits, or none; such a key of exactly 64 characters now counts as a record too, so saving it no longer restarts the learning for site pages (GitHub issue #1175).
* Fix: Links skip redirects keeps custom redirect paths, added or overridden through its filter, out of the decoded lookup used for Rank Math's exact entries, so a custom path matches only as written; the HTML precheck reads both (GitHub issue #1169).
* Fix: German, French (Canada) and Dutch (Belgium) translations of the Term list block's settings say taxonomy terms ("Begriffe", "termes", "termen") instead of conditions or terms of use, with clearer order, count and grouping labels; French (Canada) "Show" reads "Afficher" (GitHub issue #1171).
* Developers: sync starter core files to ba25117 (starter GH#619). scripts/deploy-sites.sh --apply and its dry run say when the host's deploy lock has no owner file yet, and that a run two minutes later takes it over, instead of naming a running deploy with "PID ?". In CI, the mirror.gcr.io image pull gives up after DOCKER_MIRROR_TIMEOUT seconds (default 180), so a stalled mirror falls back to Docker Hub instead of running until the job's time limit. All 83 core files match the starter (GitHub issue #1168).
* Fix: Language switcher respects TranslatePress's trp_allow_tp_to_run filter before reading languages, so excluded paths show no switcher or assets. The seoprostack_language_switcher_skip filter lets other theme callbacks suppress it without changing the setting. Missing country flags fall back to each language's flag_link, including Media Library uploads, before the existing bundled TranslatePress fallback (#1170).

= 1.3.141 =
* Fix: in French (fr_FR, fr_BE), Email protection's hidden-address text asks visitors to enable JavaScript ("activez JavaScript") instead of saying it is on ("JavaScript activé") (#1164).
* New: Media in cloud storage has a public `SEOProStack_Media_Storage::remove_local_now($id, $deadline)`, so a plugin that uploads pictures (SEO Pro Directory's import jobs) can remove one uploaded picture's local copies at once instead of waiting for the site-wide batch, which goes newest first. It takes the storage lease without waiting, checks that removal is on and not paused, that the picture is uploaded on this connection and its WebP and AVIF copies are made and not being made, then removes its files with the batch's checks (size, SHA-256, HEAD, public address, generation, changes meanwhile). It returns true when the copies are gone, false when it cannot now (call again), WP_Error `storage_kept` when a failed check keeps the files, or the service's WP_Error. A retryable service error leaves the picture for the next call; other service errors pause removal for an hour and wake the batch when the pause ends. Uploads, watermark jobs and the batch share the lease, so removals still run one at a time across the site. Removal also stops before deleting when the public address check used up the turn (GitHub issue #1146).
* Developers: sync starter core files to 1.0.50 (70b0366). Plugin Check and smoke tests share cached-image pulls and use Google's Docker Hub cache directly in CI. Optional Docker Hub login uses the starter's isolated job configuration, preserving the runner's context and proxies without touching its stored credentials. Deploy-lock cleanup removes only its own recorded lock. All 83 core files match the starter (GitHub issue #1140).
* Fix: Links skip redirects checks Rank Math and its Redirections module before using cached mappings, while still accepting custom filter mappings without Rank Math. Encoded link paths, including the HTML precheck, are decoded once for matching as Rank Math decodes request URIs; stored source patterns are not decoded. The WordPress.org feature list now includes Links skip redirects (GitHub issue #1137).
* Fix: Links skip redirects passes HTML containing possible entities to WordPress's tag processor before deciding whether an address matches. Custom filter additions and overrides match their raw path first, preserving literal plus signs and percent escapes; Rank Math still matches its decoded request path. Addresses remain subject to the existing on-site/no-query checks (#1137, review of PR #1148).

= 1.3.140 =
* New: translations: fr_FR, fr_BE, es_ES, es_MX, it_IT, pt_PT, pt_BR, nl_NL, de_AT, de_CH, pl_PL, ro_RO, lv and et, 3,291 strings each, beside nl_BE, de_DE and fr_CA. Machine translations (Google Cloud Translation v2) carry the translator comment "machine translation"; nl_NL, de_AT and de_CH copy the Belgian Dutch and German catalogues (de_CH with "ss" for "ß"), whose hand-written strings Google would otherwise replace. Without a bundled catalogue TranslatePress translated the plugin's front-end labels one fragment at a time and chose the wrong sense: on a Spanish page "Like" read "Como" ("as") and "Save" "Ahorrar" (save money). The 18 strings visitors see (the reactions, the shortlist, sharing, Close, More, Duplicate and the tally plurals) are reviewed in every locale, including nl_BE, de_DE and fr_CA ("Share" had been "Aktie" in German and "Save" "Redden" in Dutch), with short labels and the shortlist named "my list" as on the sites' headers. scripts/translations.sh compiles every languages/seoprostack-*.po instead of a fixed list (#1161).

= 1.3.139 =
* Fix: Language switcher: the list opens towards the side of its button with room. The header list still opens downwards and the footer and mobile-drawer lists upwards, but when the list does not fit there and the other side has more room it opens that way; its max-height is capped to the space it has (EDGE 14px from the screen edge, never under 120px or above the stylesheet's cap) through data-placement="up|down", so every language stays on screen and the list scrolls with its shade. = 1.3.138 =
* Fix: Media in cloud storage: upload --retry-failed retries failed uploads before background media jobs, instead of uploading nothing while those jobs have work; retry markers are kept when a bounded upload is deferred (#1152).
* New: Language switcher beside Kadence's dark mode switch (Content tab, off by default). On Kadence with TranslatePress publishing two or more languages, it prints a flag-and-code button after Kadence Pro's header, mobile and footer dark-mode switches (priority 20) that opens a list of trp_custom_language_switcher() languages with 3:2 flags (assets/flags/, MIT flag-icons), hreflang and aria-current. English uses the psdtheme_english_flag country when set; filter seoprostack_language_flag. The footer row aligns its items to the centre. Hidden where PSDTHEME_Languages already hooks the same actions (#1156).
* Fix: Media in cloud storage retries HTTP 429 and 5xx and S3 SlowDown, InternalError and ServiceUnavailable up to three attempts with jittered backoff inside the request or batch deadline. Concurrent PUT and HEAD groups retry only failed requests, preserving confirmed peers, byte caps and checked payloads. After retries an isolated removal HEAD service failure keeps that attachment's local files and lets the batch continue; two consecutive attachment failures in one run, transport failures and credential/configuration errors retain the hour-long site pause. Existing safe error messages and digest/size removal checks stay authoritative (GitHub issue #1145).
* Fix: Media in cloud storage gives an unfinished watermark job one bounded turn when its cron event is missing, before taking the storage lease; uploads resume on the next storage turn. media-storage status names the watermark wait, warns after three watermark batch budgets without recorded progress (falling back to the start time for older jobs), and reschedules a missing event. Existing watermark locks, media leases and stopped/completed jobs are respected (#1147).

Two fixes before the feature's first release: on PHP 7.4 a site's first apply no longer reports its empty custom CSS as edited-on-site and skips the template's CSS (strpos() with an empty needle fails there; strncmp() is used), and a menu item is owned by the title, URL and status WordPress stored, so a title that kses filtered on save is not taken for a site edit on the next run (GitHub issue #1131).

= 1.3.128 =
* New: wp seoprostack media-jobs start|status|stop|run remark|adopt|originals. run [--minutes=<n>] (default 60) works through a started job in the WP-CLI process, for sites without WP-Cron, and can be killed and run again. Start, stop and watermark actions wait for the shared lease (lease_wait(): up to 60 seconds in WP-CLI and cron, 5 on a web request). A turn does at most six pictures that need work and passes up to 200 that need none (already drained, adopted or outside the plan), which count as skipped. Non-autoloaded journals and indexed refusal options in the existing storage-job uninstall namespace, on the storage cron hook and atomic lease. Descending cursors, journal-before-I/O recovery, bounded tail retry passes, a rolling service-failure circuit and a free-disk check for re-marking. Adoption reuses verified handover without local restores; originals reuse private upload, digest verification and removal limits. Re-marking freezes mark identity and the mark attachment's post-date cutoff, uses kept originals, remakes format copies, uploads and removes verified local copies. Existing watermark mutations and uploads now share a nested request-owned lease; incomplete refreshes retain their original and block uploads even after interruption or stop. A new upload whose mark is queued behind a running media batch (_seoprostack_watermark_queued) is not uploaded until that watermark batch has dealt with it. A re-mark job stops, before choosing a picture, when the mark changes or Watermark pictures is off. Restoration keeps its reserved turn (GitHub issue #1069).
* Fix: Load plugins only where needed compares watched options at shutdown with fingerprints captured before their first write, per site. Fingerprints are of the stored row (wp_options option_value, or absent), not get_option(), so pre_option_/option_ filters, including those of plugins skipped on other requests, cannot hide a change. Temporary writes restored in the same request (including Kadence colours with WP-CLI --skip-themes) keep learned page kinds and add no forget-log entry; net settings changes still forget pages. Only hashes and hook names are retained, not copies of settings (GitHub issue #1074).
* Changed: Shorter readme.txt feature descriptions keep the WordPress.org readme below 9.5 KB, with room for upcoming release notes. All feature group headings, affiliate and external-service disclosures, and full release history are preserved (GitHub issue #1102).

= 1.3.127 =
* Faster: Faster editor with Kadence Blocks opens Kadence's icon picker on the press of its button (pointerdown, mouse only; the trusted click that follows is dropped so it does not close it again; keys and touch still open it on the click), draws the first 50 icons of each set instead of 150 (about a view's worth, five to a row), and shows the picker at once instead of fading in (.components-popover.kadence-icon-picker-pop-selection{opacity:1!important}; WordPress's popover slide, which carries its position, stays). Organise the admin menu's menu fitting (admin/js/seoprostack-admin-menu.js flush()) no longer checks each element inside an element a script places (inline left, right, inset or transform, such as a popover): a TreeWalker skips those subtrees, which held up a popover holding thousands of elements as it opened. Measured on the preview site in dark mode, press to picker visible in place, eight opens per page: 1,254 ms before (median), 369 ms with these changes and 207 ms with the fix below (GitHub issue #1100).
* Faster: Fixes for other plugins stops the endless SVG animations of Font Awesome's "missing icon" on screens where Lasso Lite (Simple URLs, checked with 161) loads Font Awesome 5's SVG script (surl-lasso-icons, post editor screens). EventON's tooltips (checked with EventON Lite 2.5.10) carry Font Awesome's fa class with no icon, so each became the missing icon, with four repeatCount="indefinite" animations: 550 icons and 2,200 animations on the editor, hidden in EventON's shortcode window, that Chrome still sampled every frame (40-80 ms frames; Kadence's icon picker waited 0.5 s of its 1.25 s for them, through its 100 ms debounce and React's render). An inline script after Font Awesome removes the animate elements of Font Awesome's SVGs (svg[data-fa-i2svg]) whenever Font Awesome adds fontawesome-i2svg-complete to the html element after a pass; the icons stay, still. Font Awesome's own icons have no SVG animations, and its showMissingIcons option would not help, since an element without an icon name gets the missing icon whatever it says (GitHub issue #1100).

= 1.3.126 =
* New: Custom fields to content (SEOProStack_Field_Content) can add the post's terms. Each synced post type gets field_content_{type}_terms (multi; options: the type's public taxonomies except post_format, resolved when the settings screen or save needs them, as taxonomies register after the schema is built, and kept for deselected types like the other per-type settings). The chosen taxonomies' term names (decoded, then escaped once) follow the fields, one paragraph per taxonomy, with the taxonomy's plural name as a bold label paragraph in the label-and-value format; they are part of the content hash, so a term change rewrites the content once. The block editor's REST request saves terms after save_post, so the feature also syncs on rest_after_insert_{type} for each synced type. Replaces a site's Code Snippets snippet that appended terms to ACF fields copied as name: value (GitHub issue #1097).

= 1.3.125 =
* Fix: Fluent Support email by IMAP makes the ticket the person's who wrote (GitHub issue #1093). SEOProStack_Helpdesk_Imap_Message::payload() took the customer from From only, but site email usually comes from the site's own address (FluentSMTP's Force From Email, which Amazon SES needs) with the person in Reply-To, so problem reports' tickets, and the agent replies to them, went to the site's address. When the email has a Reply-To header with exactly one address that is not the inbox's own (the IMAP user name or the business inbox's address), that address is the payload's from; otherwise From. The header itself is checked, because imap_rfc822_parse_headers() fills reply_to from From when there is none. The raw headers passed on still have the original From.
* Fix: Fixes for other plugins gives MainWP Child's Contact Support email (MainWP_Utility::send_support_mail(), checked with MainWP Child 6.2.1) a Reply-To header, on its mainwp_child_contact_support_mail_headers filter: the address the client entered, else the logged-in user's email, unless the headers have one or it is the support address itself. MainWP set the entered address only as From (header and wp_mail_from), which FluentSMTP's Force From Email replaces, so the client's address was left only in the email's text.

= 1.3.124 =
* Faster: Faster editor with Kadence Blocks (SEOProStack_Kadence_Library) has Kadence's icon picker draw the first 150 icons of each set, and 300 more each time you scroll near a set's end, instead of all 5,482 (over 22,000 elements) each time it opens. admin/js/seoprostack-kadence-icon-picker.js adds a last kadence.icon_options_names filter (priority 1000) that gives each set over 150 icons a copy whose map() stops after the icons the picker keeps (it returns each kept icon's name), so search still goes through every icon and shows the first 150 matches of each set. To draw more, the picker's set list is changed to that set and straight back, each committed at once with wp.element.flushSync (otherwise in microtasks), so the set's icons stay in place; folded sets and the scroll position are put back. On the preview site in dark mode it opens in 0.25 s instead of 0.85 s (script 35 ms instead of 360, styles 95 ms instead of 370). Checked with Kadence Blocks 3.8.0.2 and Kadence Blocks Pro 2.8.20.1: search, choosing an icon, folding a set and choosing one set (GitHub issue #1095).

It now uses getBoundingClientRect() and never ends past the Dev tab, and a ResizeObserver moves it when the tabs change width (GitHub issue #1095).

= 1.3.123 =
* New: Review links block (seoprostack/review-links, includes/class-seoprostack-review-links.php), registered by Fluent Forms by key. Server-rendered and hidden; data-form holds the keyed form's ID (formKey, default feedback-review), data-rating and data-comment its field names (default rating and comment), data-high the lowest high rating (default 4). blocks/review-links/view.js reads both fields when the form is submitted (a capturing submit listener, and a click on its submit button, before Fluent Forms resets the form) and shows the block on Fluent Forms' fluentform_submission_success for that form (the DOM event on document, detail.form, and the jQuery event on document.body, data.form; the form's own triggerHandler event does not bubble), once per submission: the high or low thanks and request (data-when), the comment in a read-only textarea with Copy review (Clipboard API in a secure context, else select-all and execCommand('copy'), announced in a polite status), and the links. Links: the Short links starter review links (SEOProStack_Short_Links::presets(): googlereview, facebookreview, trustpilotreview) that are in the live map and whose target is no longer the starter placeholder (compared without scheme, www., case and trailing slash), linked to their short address so clicks are counted. They show for every rating (review gating breaks Google's and Trustpilot's rules); a low rating gets the follow-up first and outlined buttons. With Short links off, or no link set, only the thanks shows. Wording attributes thanksHigh, thanksLow, askHigh, askLow, copyLabel. On load, a query parameter named like the rating field (?rating=5) clicks the form's radio with that value, so a feedback email's stars each link to the page with their rating ticked. The editor (blocks/review-links/index.js, after the Form by key editor script for its keyed forms) shows a summary naming the links that will show (GitHub issue #1088).
* New: SEOProStack_Form_Keys::default_inbox() on fluent_support/create_ticket_data (priority 5): ticket data with an empty mailbox_id gets FluentSupport\App\Services\Helper::getDefaultMailBox() (the is_default inbox, else the oldest). A Fluent Forms feed (fluentform_fluentsupport_feed) imported with a form cannot name a site's inbox ID, and Fluent Support would otherwise make the ticket in no inbox (GitHub issue #1088).

= 1.3.120 =
* New, on by default while Fluent Support email by IMAP is on: sent ticket emails are saved to the mailbox's Sent folder (setting helpdesk_imap_sent; SEOProStack_Helpdesk_Imap_Sent). The site's mail plugin sends Fluent Support's email (such as FluentSMTP through Amazon SES), so no copy reached the mailbox. Each email Fluent Support sends a customer from the feature's inbox is marked when its headers pass fluent_support/mail_to_customer_header (ticket created, agent reply, closed by agent, ticket created by an agent or on the customer's behalf; the ticket and its mailbox_id must be given, and be the inbox's), the mark moves to the next wp_mail() at pre_wp_mail, and after wp_mail_succeeded the global PHPMailer's getSentMIMEMessage() is kept when its From is the inbox's address. Last on shutdown, after fastcgi_finish_request() or litespeed_finish_request() where they exist so the response is not held up, the copies are appended over the same IMAP connection settings with \Seen and the sent time as INTERNALDATE, to the folder in helpdesk_imap_sent_folder or the first of Sent, Sent Items, Sent Messages, Sent Mail, INBOX.Sent, INBOX/Sent or [Gmail]/Sent Mail on the server (never created). Agent and admin notifications, FluentCRM and other site email are not copied, and sending is unchanged. Copies not saved wait in seoprostack_helpdesk_imap_sent (not autoloaded; at most 20 emails and 8 MB, others counted as not kept) and the five-minute run tries again, taking them out of the queue first, so overlapping runs do not both save one (at least once: a copy is saved twice only if that queue write fails). A copy the server refuses goes behind the others and is given up after 288 refusals (a day of runs); a failed connection counts no refusal. Removed on uninstall. The settings card and wp seoprostack helpdesk-mail status show copies saved, waiting and not kept, and the last problem (GitHub issue #1084).

= 1.3.119 =
* Fix: colours chosen from Kadence Blocks' Style Library change with Kadence Pro's dark mode, as palette colours do (GitHub issue #1083). Kadence Blocks 3.8 saves such a colour as a design token (Contrast → Strongest is var(--kb-token--primitive--color--neutral--900)) and prints its tokens on :root with fixed colours copied from palette 1 to 9, while Kadence Pro's dark mode changes the palette on the body only, so a heading with Strongest text kept its black on a palette 8 background that turned dark. Fixes for other plugins adds to its dark mode style a body.color-switch-dark rule that sets the nine palette primitives to var(--global-palette1) … var(--global-palette9), as Kadence Blocks pairs them, and repeats the semantic and preset token declarations that read them, so they resolve on the body (SEOProStack_Plugin_Fixes::kadence_token_css()). Light mode, tokens that are not palette colours, other tokens and values a breakpoint changes are left as Kadence Blocks prints them; saved blocks and both plugins' settings are not changed.
* New: Site Health detects split persistent object caches across web requests, scheduled tasks and WP-CLI, which can serve old settings and task lists. Separate non-autoloaded observations refresh hourly or on changes, are read directly from the database, expire after 7 days and are removed on uninstall. Read-only health audits do not save observations. Advice asks the host to disable the drop-in or align PHP extensions and cache configuration; nothing is changed or flushed automatically (GitHub issue #1072).

= 1.3.118 =
* New: opt-in Rank Math identity sync follows the site name, PNG logo/icon and social fallback while preserving explicit Rank Math choices. Its preset defaults Pages to WebPage without Article. wp seoprostack health reports copied Kadence/WPTT font-cache paths and missing active Rank Math module tables; --repair explicitly repairs them through local cache rebasing and Rank Math's schema routines, with post-repair checks (GitHub issue #1068).
* New, off by default: Fluent Support email by IMAP (Plugins tab, SEOProStack_Helpdesk_Imap; the reading in SEOProStack_Helpdesk_Imap_Config, _Connection, _Mime, _Message and _Reader, loaded only when used). Fluent Support Pro's email piping needs Fluent's hosted relay (apiv2.wpmanageninja.com/email-piping/), which issues a forwarding address and pushes each email to the site's mail-piping push route only for a licensed site; the ticket itself is made on the site by FluentSupportPro\...\FluentEmailPiping\ByMailHandler::processPayload(). Every five minutes (event seoprostack_helpdesk_imap, interval seoprostack_helpdesk_imap, 300 seconds) the feature reads the support mailbox over IMAP (PHP imap extension; SSL on 993, STARTTLS on 143, certificate checked) by UID after the last one read (on the first run or a new UIDVALIDITY, SINCE one day ago), up to 25 a run within 20 seconds, with FT_PEEK so nothing is marked read, and passes each email to processPayload() in the relay's payload shape (subject, body_text, messageId, inReplyTo, references, from/to/cc value lists, headers for AutoMailHandler, mime "imap-" and the first 32 hex digits of SHA-256(UIDVALIDITY:UID:Message-ID) for its duplicate check), after Fluent's fluent_support_pro/email_piping_raw_data filter. Attachments stay in the mailbox and are named in the ticket. Settings helpdesk_imap_host, helpdesk_imap_user and helpdesk_imap_mailbox; the password only from the SEOPROSTACK_HELPDESK_IMAP_PASSWORD constant or the seoprostack_helpdesk_imap_password filter. An email that throws is retried in two more runs, then passed over; a failed listing is recorded as an error and moves nothing on. The text is cut at 1 MB without splitting a character. State and the last 20 runs in seoprostack_helpdesk_imap_state (not autoloaded, no addresses or text), lock seoprostack_helpdesk_imap_lock; removed on uninstall. New wp seoprostack helpdesk-mail fetch|status.

= 1.3.117 =
* Fix: Media in cloud storage names uploads from the stored files that can matter, not the whole library (GitHub issue #1064). With local copies removed, SEOProStack_Media_Storage::unique_filename_files() (pre_wp_unique_filename_file_list) read every stored inventory, 100 at a time, for every wp_unique_filename() call (several per picture: the upload, Resize uploads' working and converted names, watermark crops). Measured on production directory stacks: 0.27 seconds a call with 1,385 stored pictures, 2.92 seconds with 15,221 and 7.24 seconds with 18,949, returning up to 567,699 names; bulk imports slowed as the library grew. WordPress tests the list only with _wp_check_existing_file_names() ("{name}-(size|scaled|rotated){ext}", case-insensitive), for the name, its "-N" numbers and other formats, so every name it can test begins with the file name's stem without a trailing "-N" (numbering_stem()). The claim index (seoprostack_media_claims) now also holds a name claim for each stored file under every hyphen-ended prefix of its name in its folder (name_hashes(), lower case), and the filter reads only the inventories under the stem's claim (stem_records(), one primary-key lookup), then returns only names that begin with the stem, local ones included. Until the index is complete for this version (rebuilt in the background after each update), or when a lookup fails, it reads every inventory as before, still returning only those names. Checked read-only against 34,669 production inventories (846,160 names): 15,480 of core's name tests answered the same.

= 1.3.116 =
* Fix: Media in cloud storage counts a picture whose upload is running as waiting, never as failed (GitHub issue #1022). Each attempt saved state failed before its first PUT, so wp seoprostack media-storage status, the settings card and the Media Library label ("Upload failed.") showed every picture in a running batch as failed, with no error. The attempt now records its retry job, then state waiting, so a run that dies part-way leaves the picture waiting and the job uploads it later; it saves failed, with the error, only when the attempt ends in a failure (upload_failed(): a refused PUT, or a restore before upload that fails, which before only set the site's last error). upload --retry-failed also requeues a waiting picture whose retry job was lost.

= 1.3.115 =
* Fix: WebP and AVIF images records a conversion only when the picture is unchanged at the end (GitHub issue #1061). WordPress saves an attachment's metadata after each size it makes, so a conversion running beside an upload, a size regeneration or a watermark refresh read only the sizes made so far, then wrote that record (_seoprostack_nextgen) and marked the picture converted after metadata_updated() had cleared the mark. Media in cloud storage uploads only listed copies, so the other sizes' copies stayed on the server, never sent: on sites removing local files during a bulk import, a storage drain never finished. metadata_updated() now changes post meta _seoprostack_nextgen_generation on every save, and metadata_committed() (added_post_meta, updated_post_meta) changes it again and clears the mark once the save is in the database, for a conversion that ended between the two; convert_attachment() reads it before the sizes and, when it, the file or the file names differ at the end, leaves the picture waiting and returns nextgen_changed. The next conversion keeps the copies made and lists every size. Background batches and wp seoprostack convert-images pass over a picture that stays waiting after an error once per run, so they no longer repeat it until their time runs out.
* Fix: Media in cloud storage converts a converted picture again before collecting its files when up-to-date WebP or AVIF copies next to them are missing from its record (SEOProStack_Nextgen_Images::record_unlisted()), from before this fix or from an older version, and uploads it with them in the next batch.

= 1.3.114 =
* Fix: Load plugins only where needed treats options named {identifier}_batch_{md5}, the queued work of the WP Background Processing library that WooCommerce and many plugins use, as records (record_write(), GitHub issue #1055). With 1.3.113 the busy single site checked addresses on schedule, until WooCommerce's privacy cleanup saved wp_1_wc_privacy_cleanup_batch_* through AJAX and the site's pages learned again, mid-run.

= 1.3.113 =
* Fix: Load plugins only where needed reads its address queue from the database (SEOProStack_Plugin_Loader::fresh_queue()) when deciding whether addresses wait: in keep_in_cron(), keep_verify(), and SEOProStack_Front_Verify::run() and take() (GitHub issue #1055). With 1.3.112 the first scheduled run checked addresses and emptied the queue; page views filled it again seconds later, but the next minute's run had been given an empty queue (or "no such option") by the persistent object cache when it started, so keep_in_cron() left the task out of the list it saved. A run could also stop the task, or take nothing, for the same reason.
* Fix: Load plugins only where needed no longer learns site pages again, and drops the addresses it checked, when plugins save records (RECORD_OPTIONS, GitHub issue #1055). Seen in sites' records of why pages learned again: seoproanalytics_alerts_checked hourly on seven sites (from AJAX and admin views), seoproanalytics_alerts, and mainwp_child_request_id_*, mwai_settings_backup_*, acp_periodic_update_*, tutor_cd_last_call_time, rank_math_indexnow_log, rank_math_sitemap_cache_files and rank_math_notifications.

= 1.3.112 =
* Fix: Load plugins only where needed's checks run on sites whose WP-Cron runs from WP-CLI (GitHub issue #1055). 1.3.111's repeating task still never ran there: page views scheduled it, and about 30 seconds later the minute's WP-CLI run (wp_doing_cron() false, so keep_verify() did not run) saved the task list it had read before, without it, while rescheduling FluentCRM and Action Scheduler. SEOProStack_Plugin_Loader::keep_in_cron() on pre_update_option_cron (registered while the feature is on, in every request) puts the task back into a list saved without it while addresses wait, at the time in the list that request read or in 30 seconds. Clearing it on purpose (empty queue, feature off, deactivation, replacing a 1.3.108 to 1.3.110 single event) goes through SEOProStack_Plugin_Loader::clear_verify(), which turns that off for the rest of the request until schedule_verify().

= 1.3.111 =
* Fix: Load plugins only where needed's seoprostack_front_verify task repeats (schedule seoprostack_front_verify, VERIFY_INTERVAL 180 seconds, registered on cron_schedules) while addresses wait, instead of single events (GitHub issue #1055). A page view's wp_schedule_single_event() was lost when a scheduled run that had read the cron option before it saved its own copy back after rescheduling its tasks; on a site whose runs take most of each minute, every one was lost (seen: added by queue_page, gone 20 seconds later before it was due, in the same save that rescheduled FluentCRM and Action Scheduler). A run reschedules a repeating task before it calls it. SEOProStack_Plugin_Loader::schedule_verify() replaces a single event left by 1.3.108 to 1.3.110; on cron requests, keep_verify() (wp_loaded) schedules it when the queue has addresses and it is missing, so it is in that run's own copy. SEOProStack_Front_Verify::run() clears it when the queue is empty, and otherwise just returns when paused, busy or learning again. With the feature off, the task clears itself (stop_verify()); deactivation clears it.

= 1.3.110 =
* Fix: Load plugins only where needed kept forgetting or never finishing its address checks on some sites (GitHub issue #1039). Changes to SEO Pro Stack's own background fields (RECORD_META_PREFIXES: _seoprostack_storage*, _seoprostack_nextgen*, _seoprostack_link_scan, _seoprostack_link_map) no longer forget the site's pages: a media storage pass writes them for thousands of attachments, from WP-CLI too, and each write made the pages learn again. queue_address() schedules seoprostack_front_verify even when the queue is full (50): a run that stopped because the pages learned again left a full queue and nothing scheduled, so checks never started again. SEOProStack_Front_Verify::normalise() drops Cloudflare Rocket Loader's per-response token (named in data-cf-settings, used in each script type and data-cf-modified- attribute), which made pages with many scripts unstable; the Expires header is compared as minutes after the response's Date ("now" when expired or within half a minute), not as a date that is new on every render.

= 1.3.108 =
* Faster: Load plugins only where needed checks each address by itself (SEOProStack_Front_Verify, includes/class-seoprostack-front-verify.php). learn_front() saves for each page kind the plugins it might also skip (propose, from front_propose(): notes pages or extends; not the kind's needs, always, core, login or KEEP_KEY plugins). start_front() keys a cookie-free address by its path and the arguments cpage, p, page, page_id and paged (front_address(); none with s), adds the skip set page_skips() finds for it in seoprostack_plugin_front_pages (held for FRONT_REVISION and the plugin set, a week at most; up to 5,000 addresses as one "|key:set:hour" string with shared sets), or queues it at shutdown after a 200 render with wp_footer (seoprostack_plugin_front_queue, at most 50). The scheduled seoprostack_front_verify task (one check at a time, 50 seconds a run, 60 seconds and 16 trials per address) renders through wp_remote_get() with ?seoprostack-verify=base|trial.TOKEN.N: verify_request() checks the one-time token in seoprostack_plugin_front_verify with hash_equals(), takes the argument out of the request, defines DONOTCACHEPAGE, never learns, and answers X-SEOProStack-Verify with the plugins skipped; a trial that fails does not flag the site. On those renders the query, pre_http_request and pre_wp_mail filters note which plugin (by call stack) wrote, sent or mailed, saved at shutdown to seoprostack_plugin_front_verify_seen. Groups are tried hint first (the kind's last passing set), halved on a difference; plugins the base render wrote with, or a kind needed elsewhere, are not tried. A render matches on the listed headers, cookie names and HTML without comments, ver arguments, nonces and white space; when it differs the base renders again, and up to 50 words that change on each render are ignored (more: unstable). Only plugins the last passing trial did skip are saved. Loopback errors count five in a row, then checks pause for an hour. Forgetting the site's pages drops seoprostack_plugin_front_pages. The Plugin loading status shows checked, skipping, average and waiting counts. Uninstall removes the four options and the hook (GitHub issue #1039).

= 1.3.105 =
* Better: Link cards keep pictures whole. Beside the text a picture was cropped to the card's height in a 32% column, so social preview pictures (1.91:1, often with words) lost their sides and logos were cut. The block has a Picture shape setting (imageShape): Its own shape (auto, the default, also for existing cards: the attachment's width over height, between 1 and 2), Wide, as in social previews (1.91:1), Square, or Fill the card's height (fill, the old look). render_block() adds is-shape-* to the wrapper and --sps-link-card-ratio to the picture's frame; beside the text the frame sits inside the card (0.75em margin, 6px corners), 8em high in every card (at most 40% of its width), centred against the text, and above the text it takes the card's width at that shape (at most 360px high).
* New: Link cards use the site's icon when the page has no picture, if it is at least 128 pixels square, and the block has Use the site's icon and Use the page's picture buttons (imageKind: page or icon). The new POST seoprostack/v1/link-card-icon route (upload_files) reads the page once through wp_safe_remote_get() with core's rest_url_details_http_request_args filter, collects apple-touch-icon and icon links (by their sizes; Apple touch icons without sizes count as 180), the msapplication-TileImage meta (WordPress's site icon, 270) and the web app manifest's icons, then /apple-touch-icon.png; it skips SVG and ICO files and icons said to be smaller, tries at most four, largest first, checks the downloaded size with wp_getimagesize() and copies the first large enough to the Media Library (reused by its address, _seoprostack_source_url). Icons show whole (contain) on the card's soft background. Fill the card's height now takes the text's height (the picture is positioned absolutely), so a square picture no longer makes the card taller.
* Fixed: with External link icons on, every Link card shows the icon after its site name. Its rule skipped links with a picture (:has(img)), and on cards without one the ::after became a flex item alone at the bottom left. Link cards mark the site name with sps-external-link-after; the External link icons stylesheet puts the icon on that element's ::after inside a marked link, and leaves the link's own ::after off when it has one, and when the link or a container has sps-no-external-icon (GitHub issue #1036).
* New, on by default: Kadence author box fields (SEOProStack_Author_Fields, Admin tab, kadence_author_fields). The Kadence theme's author box (template-parts/content/entry_author.php) reads user meta occupation, facebook, twitter, instagram, threads, youtube, flickr, vimeo, linkedin, pinterest, dribbble, amazon, medium, goodreads and bookbub, but only Kadence Starter Templates (KadenceWP\KadenceStarterTemplates\Author_Meta) adds fields for them. While get_template() is kadence and that class is not loaded, show_user_profile and edit_user_profile (20) draw an Author box section with the same keys for people who can edit_posts, when the current user can edit_user; keys already in wp_get_user_contact_methods() (Rank Math's facebook and twitter) are left out. personal_options_update and edit_user_profile_update save only with the section's seoprostack_author_fields nonce: Occupation through sanitize_text_field(), links through esc_url_raw(); an empty field deletes its meta (GitHub issue #1042).
* Fix: Dark mode in wp-admin covers Fluent Security's Two-Factor Authentication card (TwoFaProfileHandler, #fls2fa) on the profile and edit user screens, coloured in a <style id="fls2fa-styles"> block printed with the card: the card, rows, names and meta, status badges and icons by tone (on, warn, off), Turn off and Remove buttons, banners, the no-JavaScript panel, the set-up dialogs with their setup key and recovery codes, and the dialog backdrop. The QR code keeps its white ground (GitHub issue #1042).

= 1.3.102 =
* New: FluentCRM's email designer (checked with FluentCRM 3.2.6) gets a button in its header, after Email Preview, and one in Email Preview, after the desktop, tablet and mobile buttons, that shows the email as Apple Mail, Outlook and Gmail may show it in their own dark mode, in any admin colour mode. FluentCRM's emails set no dark colours, so those apps darken them: admin/js/seoprostack-mail-dark.js reads each element's computed colours in the email's frame (iframe[name=editor-canvas] in the designer, .fcrm_email_preview_shell iframe in Email Preview) and adds one stylesheet to that frame's <head>, of selectors by position from <body> with two ids' weight: light backgrounds and borders turn dark and dark text light, keeping their hue; pictures and colours already dark stay. It is made again as the email changes (MutationObserver, after 250 ms) and stops watching a page its frame has replaced. The blocks, and the email that is saved and sent, are unchanged. The choice is kept in localStorage (seoprostack-mail-dark), and the designer and the screen around it follow each other through the storage event. The button's name has no "Preview": FluentCRM hides the header's buttons with it in their name. SEOProStack_Admin_Whole_Theme::fluent_editor_head() prints the script in the designer, fluent_mail_dark() enqueues it on FluentCRM's screens. In Dark, FluentCRM's "Loading editor" panel over the designer (.fcrm_editor_loader_blocks_preview, a fixed #ffffffe6) takes Dark's canvas, border and muted text (GitHub issue #1030).

= 1.3.101 =
* Faster: Ask before licence checks keeps Fluent Boards Pro's __fbs_plugin_license_lc, which its GPL Vault build (2.2.2) saves with time() in its main file on every load; nothing in Fluent Boards or Fluent Boards Pro reads it. SEOProStack_Option_Writes::KNOWN rules can now name the whole option as the timestamp (time '', kept while stored and new values are both numbers), read the licence state from another option (from: __fbs_plugin_license, status valid) and take a date string as the expiry (until, through strtotime(); still at least a week away). Like the other known options it is kept from the start of the request when the must-use file of Load plugins only where needed runs (GitHub issue #1025).

= 1.3.100 =
* Fix: Dark mode in wp-admin covers FluentCRM's email designer (checked with FluentCRM 3.2.6), the block editor page it shows in a frame on campaign, template, sequence and automation email screens (post-new.php?fluent_crm_block_editor=1). That page is printed at admin_init and exits, so it has no admin_head and no enqueued styles of other plugins: SEOProStack_Admin_Whole_Theme::fluent_editor_head() on fluent_crm/block_editor_head (20, after FluentCRM's stylesheet) prints the mode script and, in Dark or System, the stylesheet. admin/js/seoprostack-whole-theme.js keeps the frame's sps-theme-*, sps-mode-dark and sps-dark classes in step when the mode changes on the screen around it (body.fcrm_custom_editor), adding the stylesheet on the first switch to dark. WordPress's editor parts take the stylesheet's block editor rules; FluentCRM's own colour properties (--fc-*, --fcrm-compose-btn-*, --fcrm-sidebar-*) take the palette, and rules under html.sps-dark body.fcrm_custom_editor cover its buttons, Style panels, Global colors list, smartcode, link and AI writer pop-ups and the footer settings dialog; the email canvas is a frame of its own and keeps its colours (GitHub issue #1023).

= 1.3.99 =
* Faster: Fixes for other plugins. Kadence Blocks Pro's query blocks (checked with 2.8.20.1) run their query through rest_do_request() for GET /wp/v2/kadence_query/query while the page renders, and the first rest_get_server() fires rest_api_init for every plugin (about 450 ms of a 700 ms query block on a network with WooCommerce, MCP Adapter and Fluent's apps). On pre_render_block (PHP_INT_MAX) of a kadence/query-* block whose query has not run ($kbp_ql_id set, no $kb_query_rest_responses entry), with no REST server running, $wp_rest_server is set to SEOProStack_Kadence_Query_Server (includes/class-seoprostack-kadence-query-server.php), a WP_REST_Server holding only that route with Kadence's own callback (query), permission_callback (__return_true) and args (get_query_params()) from the kadence_query post type's REST controller. Its dispatch() clears the global first, sends any other route or method to the full server, and blocks rendered inside the query do not stand it in again; render_block (PHP_INT_MAX) clears it after each block. REST requests are not changed. The Kadence theme (checked with 1.5.2) reads get_theme_mod() over 1,300 times as it builds a page's CSS, each decoding the whole theme_mods_{stylesheet} option (37 KB, 250 ms per page on one site): pre_option_theme_mods_{stylesheet} (PHP_INT_MAX, Kadence or a child theme) returns the value decoded once per site and stored string in wp_load_alloptions(), through the option's own option_{name} filters; a non-autoloaded option is left to WordPress (GitHub issue #1020).

= 1.3.98 =
* New: Watermark pictures has a Never watermark box in each picture's details (attachment_fields_to_edit, priority 11; the Media Library, the editors' media windows and the attachment edit screen), for pictures the user can edit, shown while the feature is on or when the box is already ticked. It stores post meta _seoprostack_watermark_skip = '1' (SEOProStack_Watermark_Images::SKIP_META; skipped()), saved by attachment_fields_to_save only when the form carried the box (a hidden seoprostack_watermark_skip_shown field), so another screen's save never clears it. unmarkable() refuses such a picture (watermark_never) before the seoprostack_watermark_attachment filter, so upload marking, the row and bulk actions, whole-library batches and wp seoprostack watermark-images skip it, and add() refuses it before taking an existing mark off to mark again: a marked picture keeps its mark until Remove watermark. Code that adds a site's own photographs can set the meta on add_attachment, before the sizes are made and marked, instead of a site filter (GitHub issue #1018).

= 1.3.97 =
* New, on by default: Fluent Forms by key (SEOProStack_Form_Keys, Content tab, fluentforms_keys; keys stored and found by SEOProStack_Form_Key_Store, the modal in SEOProStack_Form_Modal, policy links in SEOProStack_Form_Policy_Links, WP-CLI in SEOProStack_Form_Keys_CLI). A Fluent form carries a key in its form meta (seoprostack_form_key: lower-case words joined by hyphens), and the published form holding a key on the current site is shown for it (the oldest when two share one), so pages copied between sites or made from a template never name a form ID. Blocks: seoprostack/form (Form by key: formKey, chosen in the sidebar from the site's keyed forms passed as window.seoprostackFormKeys, and panel; ServerSideRender preview) and seoprostack/form-modal (Form modal: formKey, modalId, title, intro; a Placeholder summary in the editor). Shortcodes [seoprostack_form key panel] and [seoprostack_form_modal key id title intro]. The modal is Kadence Blocks Pro's Modal block markup (kadence-block-pro-modal, kt-modal-overlay, kt-modal-container) printed once per ID in wp_footer at priority 9, with Kadence Blocks Pro's kt-modal-init script and modal stylesheet under the block's own handles, so links to #ID open it (Kadence's modal-trigger links, Modal links) and a page arriving with #ID opens it; without Kadence Blocks Pro the form stays in place in a .seoprostack-form-panel with the same ID. enqueue_empty_block_content_assets keeps the Form modal block's assets, as WordPress 6.9 dequeues those of a block that prints nothing, which left the modal's form unstyled and stuck loading. assets/form-keys.js: a click on the overlay is stopped when the press began in the panel (MicroModal closes on any click whose target is the overlay); an opened Choices.js list (showDropdown) scrolls into view; Tab wraps between the first and last visible focusable nodes, as Kadence's trap wraps at hidden later steps of a multi-step form. assets/form-keys.css: base panel, modal, title and introduction styles at the least weight that beats Kadence Blocks Pro's own (div:where(.seoprostack-form-modal) .kt-modal-container), so any theme rule naming the classes wins, and an in-flow open list in the modal. fluentform/submission_inserted (priority 20) fires seoprostack_form_submitted($key, $data, $form, $entry_id) for keyed forms. WP-CLI: wp seoprostack form-keys list [--copy-from=<meta-key>], set <form-id> <key>, set --copy-from=<meta-key> (every form with that meta and no key yet), unset <form-id>. Starter data fluentform_forms items take key. Editors see a notice where a key has no published form; visitors see nothing (GitHub issue #1016).
* New, on by default: Policy links in forms open a new tab (fluentforms_policy_tabs). Links in Fluent Forms' terms, GDPR, checkbox and custom HTML fields (fluentform/rendering_field_html_*, priority 25) to the privacy policy page, privacy-policy, cookie-policy, terms, terms-and-conditions, terms-of-service or terms-of-use get target="_blank", rel="noopener", the seoprostack-new-tab class and "(opens in a new tab)" screen-reader text; links already opening a new tab are left alone. Filter: seoprostack_form_policy_paths.

= 1.3.93 =
* Fix: Media in cloud storage deletes an attachment's retry job when removed_elsewhere() refuses its upload (its local files were removed while another storage connection was set, so the bucket objects are its only copies). upload_attachment() returned before it replaced or removed the job, so the due job ran and was refused in every batch, each time counting against process_jobs()'s retry limit; once that was reached, upload_batch() skipped pictures waiting for their first upload. collision_failed() already deleted its job, and the failed-upload recovery sweep expects refusals to have none (GitHub issue #1003).
* Developers: all 79 starter core files match WP Plugin Starter 4efe5a6 (1.0.45 and later). scripts/sync-core.sh stops (exit 2) when it cannot tell the plugin's GitHub repository (no GitHub Plugin URI header and no GitHub origin remote) instead of writing core files with links to a repository that does not exist; --repo OWNER/REPO gives it. The SYNC_PAT comment in .github/workflows/star-history.yml says it the right way round: GitHub shows when each star was given only to the repository's collaborators. scripts/preflight-release.sh accepts --from DIR for its core-files comparison and warns when README.md's aidevops-managed block lacks its Star History or Built with aidevops heading (GitHub issue #1001).

= 1.3.92 =
* Fix: watermark-images --dry-run does not restore removed cloud files or write a cursor. --stale compares mark-file and settings signatures, including legacy records as stale; --stale and --marked-only resume newest first, with --from-start to reset. Re-marking verifies and reuses the kept original, preserving its backup, size, SHA-256 and private-bucket identity rather than deleting and uploading unchanged originals (#996).

= 1.3.91 =
* Fix: Fit the menu to its names measured text kept for screen readers. room() in admin/js/seoprostack-admin-menu.js took a Range over the whole entry, which includes the text inside core's 1px, clipped .screen-reader-text span; with sps-menu-measure's white-space: nowrap that text lies on one line, so while a comment awaited moderation the Comments entry ("Comments <span class="awaiting-mod">…<span class="comments-in-moderation-text screen-reader-text">1 Comment in moderation</span>") needed 264px and the menu grew to 267px on every screen (206px needed, on two live sites). The new drawn() unions the rectangles of the entry's text nodes and elements, skipping .screen-reader-text and any element taken out of the flow and clipped (clip or clip-path), through unseen(). The remembered spsmw width, more than FIT_SLACK above the new need, is replaced on the next admin screen (GitHub issue #994).
* Fix: Fluent Forms colours also applies in the block editor's canvas, so a form's preview in the editor has the page's label, help text, input and upload colours instead of Fluent Forms' defaults. boot() no longer returns in wp-admin: there it hooks editor_styles() to enqueue_block_assets at priority 100, after a theme's canvas styles, as the front-end CSS prints with Fluent Forms' late stylesheet. editor_styles() registers the source-less handle seoprostack-fluentforms-colours with the same CSS (now css()) once and enqueues it, so WordPress's iframe asset collection, which runs enqueue_block_assets again, gets it too. The front end is unchanged (GitHub issue #992).
