Long ordered lists of choices stay one column, and sync-core.sh supports add-ons (scripts/core-skip.txt) (GitHub issue #986).

= 1.3.90 =
* Fix: Media in cloud storage checks a picture's files against other attachments' claims (GH#956) without reading every stored inventory. conflicting_files() called stored_records(), which unserialized every _seoprostack_storage and _seoprostack_storage_removed value, once per picture and again per upload group: 1.7 to 4.6 seconds a call on a stack with 17,300 inventories, so uploads slowed as the library grew. It now reads only candidates, then applies the same comparison, so every GH#956 case is still refused: the same relative path under any destination, and the same key, including an unconfirmed attempt, under the current one. Candidates come from the new claim index, the {prefix}seoprostack_media_claims table (claim binary(16), the MD5 of each stored relative path and of each key with its destination's signature; post_id), through its primary key. The added_post_meta and updated_post_meta hooks index each inventory as it is saved, before the saver checks it; rows are only added, as a stale row only makes another candidate, and deleting an attachment removes its rows. run_batch() creates the table and fills it for up to 5 seconds a batch, again after each version change, since an earlier version may have saved inventories without indexing them. Until the index is complete, or if a query on it fails, the check searches the serialized values a page at a time through the meta_key index, unserializing nothing (about 0.5 seconds on the same stack). A MySQL LIKE pre-filter was measured first and was slower than the old scan (1.2 to 11.9 seconds), as matching runs through the column's collation. collisions and collisions --repair keep their full scan. Uninstall drops the table; its state option seoprostack_storage_job_claims goes with the other jobs (GitHub issue #981).
* Developers: README.md ends like the other wpallstars plugins: Built with AI names SEO Pro Analytics and SEO Pro Cookie Consent, and the aidevops-managed Star History and Built with aidevops sections follow the License, with docs/assets/star-history.svg and .github/workflows/star-history.yml.
* Developers: all 79 starter core files match WP Plugin Starter 2b008cc. The Read Me tab leaves out the aidevops-managed Star History and Built with aidevops sections (their chart does not ship), and scripts/preflight-release.sh has a README.md ending section that warns when Built with AI, License or the managed block is missing or out of order. The single-site deploy diagnostics fix (#964) is now in the starter, so its copy here no longer shows as changed here; RELEASING.md documents the FAIL reason, and Scorecard's SARIF uploader is pinned to v4.39.1 (GitHub issue #982).

= 1.3.89 =
* Fix: Media in cloud storage purges page caches after Remove local copies deletes a picture's files. remove_file() marks the attachment once its local file is deleted, and run_batch() (in its finally block, after the lease is released) purges the parent post of each marked attachment once, and the attachment's own page unless wp_attachment_pages_enabled is off, through the new SEOProStack_Page_Cache::purge_posts(): LiteSpeed Cache's litespeed_purge_post action (which also purges the archives its settings list; under WP-CLI LiteSpeed queues it for the next request), or for an attachment, which litespeed_purge_post skips (status inherit), litespeed_purge with its post tag Po.<ID> and WPO_Page_Cache::delete_single_post_cache() when WP-Optimize's page cache is on, plus the new seoprostack_page_cache_purge_post action for other caches. Nothing is purged when no file was deleted, and the batch never purges everything. Other pages that show a picture without being its parent keep their cache entries until they expire (GitHub issue #976).
* Fix: Media in cloud storage no longer deletes a retry job before its upload attempt; the attempt replaces it or removes it on completion, so an early return cannot strand a failed attachment. wp seoprostack media-storage upload --retry-failed first requeues failed image attachments of the current destination without a retry job (retry_stranded(), paged by post ID through the primary key, newest first), skipping collision refusals (marked collision_refused, including legacy refusals detected from collision_source or live shared claims) and pictures removed under another connection. collisions --repair clears the refusal mark when it queues the owner (GitHub issue #975).
* Changed: screenshots 1 to 3 (.wordpress-org/screenshot-N.png and the GitHub build's admin/images/screenshot-N.webp) retaken on 1.3.89 with default settings.

= 1.3.88 =
* Fix: Simpler block editor → Advanced Text for new lines (`editor_tidy_kadence_text`) defaults to off, and settings version 35 (`SEOProStack_Editor_Tidy::migrate()`) saves it off once on every site. While on, Kadence's Advanced Text was the default block, and it has none of core Paragraph's prefix transforms ("* " or "- " to a list, "1. " to a numbered list, "## " to a heading), so people adding content lost their "* " bullets. Kadence blocks first is unchanged: core List and Heading stay out of the inserter but their transforms still work from a Paragraph. A site that saved Kadence's own "Set Advanced Text as default block" switch on keeps it (GitHub issue #978).
* Fix: admin Dark mode for Kadence Blocks 3.8 block settings (`admin/css/seoprostack-whole-theme.css`): the inspector tabs (`.kadence-blocks-inspector-tabs`, #edf2f7 with the open tab #fff), Normal / Hover tabs (`.kt-hover-tabs`), `.kb-color-control__trigger`, `.kadence-token-field__trigger` and its lists, `.kb-border-control__style-preview`, `.kadence-single-border-control-wrap`, spacing labels (`.input-setting-toggle-btn`), sub-panel titles, `.kt-sidebar-settings-spacer`, the icon picker's search bar and icons, the token pickers' #fcf9e8 notes and the Custom CSS Ace editor (`ace-tm`, its code colours too) were light. They no longer depend on the generated dark fallback stylesheet catching them (GitHub issue #978).

= 1.3.87 =
* Changed: Media in cloud storage uploads up to 20 files together and uses the same request cap for removal's HEADs. seoprostack_media_storage_concurrency accepts 1–32 (6 restores the earlier request limit). PUT groups sum file sizes against seoprostack_media_storage_group_bytes (default 32 MiB), reduced to a quarter of available PHP memory after a 16 MiB reserve; an eligible file larger than the cap goes alone. The 16 MiB single-file limit stays. Attempted keys remain durable before PUT, successful peers remain confirmed on failure, retries and whole-attachment publication are unchanged, and groups respect the batch deadline. In a PUT group given less than the usual 20 seconds by the batch deadline, a file whose request was still running when that time ran out waits for the next turn like a budget deferral instead of failing into the hourly retry; transport errors that came earlier, and HTTP errors, remain failures. Signed read-back GETs remain single-object checks, outside the normal upload path (GitHub issue #971).
* Fix: Media in cloud storage, when a private originals bucket is set after public uploads finished, uploads only each kept original for an attachment whose public inventory is complete and confirmed under the current signature. It no longer restores and re-uploads every public file first, which on sites with Remove local copies downloaded the whole library again. Public URLs keep serving while the original waits; an original's retry backoff is respected and cannot starve older uploads, and a concurrent edit or replacement still queues a full upload (GitHub issue #968).
* Fix: Fixes for other plugins sets LiteSpeed's noabort variable for every request, not just WordPress cron and Action Scheduler. Visitors leaving pages no longer interrupt queries and leave "Commands out of sync" errors at shutdown. Requests keep PHP's and LiteSpeed's normal time limits; abandoned requests still use CPU, reduced for anonymous pages served by LiteSpeed Cache's page cache. Existing marked .htaccess blocks update on the next main-site admin request; the main site's switch still controls the shared network file, and switching off or deactivating still removes the block (GitHub issue #967).
* Fix: Media in cloud storage resumes adopt and migrate batches from separate, non-autoloaded destination-bound cursors saved under the existing lease. Commands report the next position and clear their cursor at inventory end to revisit deferred or failed records on the next pass. --from-start ignores the cursor; dry runs and explicit --attachment runs never change it. Existing verification, budget and compare-and-swap behavior is unchanged (GitHub issue #965).

= 1.3.86 =
* Fix: Email protection also hides the address in a `mailto:` link's other attributes. The block editor's link control writes `data-id="mailto:…"`, and a `title` or `aria-label` may repeat the address, so 1.3.85 still showed it in the HTML. On an email link, every attribute other than `href` whose value holds an address or `mailto:` is removed; `title`, `aria-label`, `aria-description` and `data-*` ones are XOR-encoded together as JSON in `data-sps-attrs`, and `assets/js/email-protection.js` restores only those names, never event handlers or styles. Any other such attribute is dropped (GitHub issue #959).

= 1.3.85 =
* New, on by default: Email protection (`SEOProStack_Email_Protection`, Security tab, `email_protection`). Email addresses in text and `mailto:` links rendered through `the_content`, `widget_text`, `widget_text_content`, `widget_block_content`, `render_block` and `wp_nav_menu_items` become placeholders (`<span class="sps-email" data-sps-e="…">` with a `<noscript>` note, and `href="#sps-email"` with `data-sps-mailto` on links, keeping their classes and inner markup), XOR-encoded with one key per response. `assets/js/email-protection.js`, deferred and enqueued only when a page has a placeholder, restores the text and links. Entity-encoded addresses (such as `antispambot()`'s) are caught too. Comments, `<script>`, `<style>`, `<textarea>`, `<title>`, `<noscript>` and other attributes are left alone, as are the admin, feeds, Ajax, REST and JSON responses and `wp_mail`; stored content does not change. Works without Cloudflare's Email Address Obfuscation (and alongside it); scrapers that run JavaScript can still read addresses. The `seoprostack_email_protection_skip` filter leaves a context plain (GitHub issue #927).
* Fix: Media in cloud storage reserves offloaded filenames and refuses conflicting uploads. The new wp seoprostack media-storage collisions command reports shared paths and keys; --repair renames earlier attachments, copies only local bytes matching the recorded SHA-256, and requeues failed owners once their conflicts are cleared, including across repair pauses. Shared bucket objects stay untouched; missing or mismatched original bytes must be re-added (GitHub issue #956).
* Fix: Kadence Pro dark mode re-declares the four secondary button colour variables on body.color-switch-dark when their Customizer values reference palette slots. Text, hover text and backgrounds resolve against the dark palette instead of the light root; literal colours, transparent and light mode stay unchanged (GitHub issue #942).
* Fix: admin Dark mode: Debug Log Manager's Latest Errors dashboard widget (`#debug_log_manager_widget`) kept its light colours, which it prints in a `<style>` block inside the widget (`get_dashboard_widget_entries()`, 2.5.3), so the stylesheet copies did not reach them: the `#f6f7f7` footer behind Go to Debug Log Manager and odd entries, `#e6e7e7` lines and `#50575e` source links (`#2271b1` on hover). The footer is now the raised surface with muted text, the entries take the stripe and border colours, and the links are muted, with the light accent on hover and focus (`admin/css/seoprostack-whole-theme.css`) (GitHub issue #947).

= 1.3.84 =
* New: Media in cloud storage optionally uploads Watermark pictures' kept originals to SEOPROSTACK_MEDIA_STORAGE_ORIGINALS_BUCKET, a separate private bucket. Signed downloads check size and SHA-256 for unmarking, re-marking and cropping; verified local copies go only with Remove local copies on. CLI counts, explicit restore and durable private-object deletion retries are included. Without the constant, originals stay local (GitHub issue #943).
* Fix: Private-original uploads retry separately from confirmed public media; completed inventories no longer restart, failed inventory jobs retain their cursor, and durable deletion jobs let replaced pictures discard stale watermark records.
* New: Avatars without Gravatar (`SEOProStack_Avatar_Privacy`) has Copy pictures from Gravatar (`avatar_gravatar_import`, on by default at the owner's request): the cron hook `seoprostack_avatar_gravatar_import` takes 20 people at a time who can write posts (`capability__in` `edit_posts`) and are due (user meta `seoprostack_avatar_gravatar` missing, or a retry time now due), downloads their 512px picture from secure.gravatar.com by the SHA-256 hash of their email address, with no default image (404 instead) and the site's avatar rating, and stores it with the profile picture code (marked `gravatar`). A 404 or an unreadable picture records 0 (not asked again until the email address changes, `profile_update`); a failed request or unwritable folder is retried a day later. Pictures uploaded or removed here, and Avatar Privacy's, are never replaced. New people and role changes schedule a batch; switching the setting off unschedules it (GitHub issue #945).
* Fix: Avatars without Gravatar turns `show_avatars` on once in settings version 34 where the feature is on (new installs too): with it off, `get_avatar()` returned false and Kadence's author meta printed an empty avatar box. Version 34 also schedules the first Gravatar copies (GitHub issue #945).
* Fix: Avatars without Gravatar stops Kadence Pro's Local Gravatars (and the Local Gravatars plugin) through their `get_local_gravatars_max_process_time` and `get_local_gravatars_fallback_url` filters, so avatar addresses stay as printed instead of being downloaded again into `wp-content/gravatars/` (a loopback copy of each local file, and a broken address when a download failed). Kadence Pro's setting is not changed (GitHub issue #945).

= 1.3.83 =
* New: Social sharing (`SEOProStack_Social_Sharing`) has Your own platforms (`social_sharing_custom`, a lines setting): `Name | Brand icon | share address` or `Name | share address`, where the address is http(s) and must hold `{url}` (a line without it is skipped), with `{title}` (and `{media}`) optional, encoded as for the built-in networks. Each becomes the network `custom-{name}` (`custom_platform()` reads a line, `custom_networks()` the list, added to `network_options()` after the built-in ones, so Share on orders it with them); the Brand icon, or the name, is looked up with `SEOProStack_Brand_Icons::find()` for the logo and its colour (`brand_color()`), and without a match the button draws a share symbol of its own in the text colour. A platform added from the settings screen is ticked at the end of Share on (`settings_changed()` notes it, `setting_saved()` adds it once the save's lock is released); one taken off the list is no longer drawn (GitHub issue #938).
* New: Social sharing's Place has Before and after the content (`both`), and Place on each post type (`social_sharing_type_places`, an open multi of `before-{type}` and `after-{type}` for the registered types under Show on other than products, and any type with a place ticked) gives a type its own places (`places()`); types with none follow Place. Kadence Simple Share's `both` location is now imported as both (GitHub issue #938).
* Change: Social sharing's defaults are every network (Facebook, X, LinkedIn, WhatsApp, Reddit, Pinterest, Email, Copy page link), on `post`, `spd_question`, `product` and `business`, as large outlined circles; place (after), colour (brand), text (none), tooltips (above) and window (pop-up) are unchanged. Settings version 33 (`new_defaults()`) gives them to sites where Social sharing is off and those four settings all still hold 1.3.82's defaults (`OLD_DEFAULTS`); sites using it, or with any of them changed, keep their choices (GitHub issue #938).
* Fix: Social sharing's outlined circles have a 2px ring instead of 1px, which some screens drew unevenly in dark mode, thicker at the top, bottom and sides. Normal (32px) buttons are 12px apart instead of 8px, with a 44px touch target each (`a::before`, 6px around the button), so on a phone a tap finds one button without touching the next; large buttons keep 8px (GitHub issue #938).
* Fix: Watermark pictures records up to 100 pictures that failed in a run, lists them (and how many more) in its settings, the Media Library's notices and the WP-CLI summary, and retries only those, with the same operation (GitHub issue #936).
* New (GitHub builds): settings and Site Health report host background-update blocks. The off-by-default “Keep GitHub auto-updates on when the host turns background updates off” allows packages served by the shared GitHub plugin and theme updaters, excludes core and other plugins/themes, and retains update-delay holds and WordPress safety checks. Saved auto-update choices and translation filters are unchanged (GitHub issue #934).

= 1.3.82 =
* New, on by default: Modal links (`SEOProStack_Modal_Links`, Links tab, `modal_links`). A link to `#id` on the same page, where that element is a modal or drawer (`<dialog>`, `role="dialog"` or `role="alertdialog"`, `aria-modal="true"`, Kadence's `.popup-drawer` or a Kadence Modal block, `.kadence-block-pro-modal`), opens it: the modal's own opener outside it (`[data-toggle-target="#id"]`, `[data-modal-open]`, `[data-micromodal-trigger]` or `[aria-controls]`) is clicked, so Kadence opens `#login-drawer`, `#search-drawer`, `#mobile-drawer` and `#cart-drawer` with its own focus handling and scroll lock; otherwise a Kadence Modal block opens with `MicroModal.show()` and Kadence Blocks Pro's settings (`kt-modal-init.js`), and a `<dialog>` gets a cancelable `seoprostack:open-modal` event, then `showModal()`. Before, Kadence opened its drawers only from its header toggles, and its Modal block only from links with the `modal-trigger` class at page load (`initializeMicroModal()`), so an editor's text link to `#login-drawer` only jumped to a hidden element. The click listener runs in the capture phase, before Kadence's smooth scrolling for same-page anchors would scroll to the hidden element. A page loaded with such a hash opens it after `load`; one already open is left as it is. Other links to IDs (anchors, tabs, accordions, Cookie Consent's `#cookie-settings`), clicks with a modifier key, `download` links and links with another `target` keep their behaviour. Links that open a modal get `aria-haspopup="dialog"` and `aria-controls`. The `seoprostack_modal_links_selectors` filter adds selectors for other kinds of modal (passed as `window.seoprostackModalLinks.selectors`); each is checked when the script starts and one the browser cannot parse is dropped alone, so it cannot stop the built-in selectors matching. Elements matched only by `role="dialog"`, `role="alertdialog"`, `aria-modal="true"` or a filtered selector have no generic way to open, so they open only through their own opener: without one, the click follows the hash as before and a page loaded with it leaves them closed. The script, `assets/modal-links.js`, loads in the footer of front-end pages (GitHub issue #929).

= 1.3.81 =
* New: Social sharing (`SEOProStack_Social_Sharing`) has a `copy` network, Copy page link: a link to the page itself (`data-sps-copy`), drawn with a chain link of its own (`svg()`), that copies `get_permalink()` with the Clipboard API, or a hidden textarea and `execCommand('copy')` where that is not available (plain http), and as a last resort shows the address in a prompt. For two seconds its name and tooltip say “Link copied” (`sps-share--copied`, shown without `data-tip` too) and a `role="status"` span tells screen readers. The inline script now loads when Copy page link is chosen as well as for pop-up windows. `copy` joins the default networks (`facebook`, `x`, `copy`), and settings version 32 adds it after stored networks (GitHub issue #931).
* Fix: Social sharing's outline style set `color:#fff` on every hovered button, so the black and white buttons (`sps-share--mono`: X and Email), which fill with `currentColor` and draw the logo in `--global-palette9`, were white on white in light mode. Their hover keeps `color:inherit`, so the circle is the text colour and the logo contrasts in light and dark mode, as in the filled style. Found on an owner site after the switch from Kadence Simple Share (GitHub issue #931).

= 1.3.80 =
* Fix: Image loading (`SEOProStack_Image_Loading`) adds `data-no-lazy="1"` to the pictures it loads straight away: the site logo, pictures with `fetchpriority="high"`, the first content pictures (`image_loading_eager`) and pictures matching the exclusion list. It does this in `wp_get_attachment_image_attributes` and `wp_content_img_tag`, so pages are still not rewritten. Page-rewriting lazy loaders skip pictures with that attribute: LiteSpeed Cache 7.9.1 checks it in `src/media.cls.php`, and WP Rocket does too. Before, LiteSpeed Cache's Lazy Load Images replaced even the `fetchpriority="high"` logo with a `data:image/svg+xml` placeholder (`data-lazyloaded`, real address in `data-src`) for visitors who were not logged in, so the logo flashed on every page change. Core's `custom-logo` class joins the default exclusions (`keywords()`), which covers Kadence's main and mobile (`extra-custom-logo`) logos, so logos also lose `loading="lazy"` (GitHub issue #925).

= 1.3.79 =
* Fix: Social sharing keeps the networks' order. Kadence Simple Share's `kt_share` networks are imported in its order (`import_values()`), and `social_sharing_networks` is an `ordered` multi setting, so the order is stored as given. Settings version 31 (`keep_order()`) puts the stored networks in Kadence Simple Share's order where they are the same networks as its `kt_share` (still there after it is deactivated), as 1.3.78 imported them in this feature's order; a different choice is left alone. Share on lists the chosen networks first, with handles to drag them or move them with the arrow keys. The buttons draw Font Awesome's marks from Brand icons (`facebookf`, `xtwitter`, `linkedinin`, `pinterestp`, `redditalien`; WhatsApp keeps Simple Icons'), the letter or symbol alone as Kadence Simple Share drew them, in the brand colours from the Simple Icons entries; Simple Icons' Facebook and others are round badges themselves, which looked busy inside a round button. Found on marcusquinn.com after the switch from Kadence Simple Share (GitHub issue #923).
* Developers: core files from the starter (wpallstars/wp-plugin-starter-template-for-ai-coding#538, `scripts/sync-core.sh`): a multi setting can take `ordered` (true) to keep its chosen values in the order given (`SEOProStack_Settings::sanitize_multi()`), and its panel lists them first with drag handles (`.sps-checkbox-row`, `.sps-checkbox__handle`, `data-sps-ordered`), saved at once. The admin script now depends on `jquery-ui-sortable`. `SEOProStack_Settings::same()` takes the setting key and compares an ordered field in order, so a change that only reorders the networks counts on the network screen and in Make every site the same (starter #540, from CodeRabbit's review of #924).

= 1.3.78 =
* New: Social sharing (`SEOProStack_Social_Sharing`, `includes/features/class-seoprostack-social-sharing.php`, off by default) replaces Kadence Simple Share. Facebook, X, LinkedIn, Pinterest, Reddit, WhatsApp and Email share links before or after the content of chosen post types (`the_content`, priority 100, main query, singular, published, no password) and on WooCommerce products (`woocommerce_share`), drawn with Brand icons as inline SVG in round Filled or Outline buttons, Normal (32 px) or Large (48 px), with optional text, tooltips above or below from each link's `aria-label`, and a pop-up window (inline script, only on pages with the buttons; blocked pop-ups fall back to `target="_blank"`) or new tab. Colour: brand colours, or one Kadence palette colour for every button, with the logo colour picked per palette group so it contrasts in light and dark mode; X and Email take the text colour. One inline style, no files, `rel="noopener nofollow"`. The per-post choice is Kadence Simple Share's `_kt_share_control` meta (registered for REST, Default, Show or Hide in the summary, `admin/js/seoprostack-social-sharing.js`), and `[kadence_simple_share]` is registered when nothing else has it. Its `kt_share` settings are imported while it is active (`migrate()`; Twitter → X, styles 3 to 5 → Outline, after/both/fixed bars → after); its unused options (checked on 28 sites) are not offered. While `Kadence_Simple_Share` is loaded, the buttons, style and editor control stand aside (`replaced_loaded()`), so pages never show two rows; deactivating it hands over with the same settings and per-post choices (GitHub issue #921).
* New: Simpler block editor → View in this tab: core's View button is named "View Page in a new tab" ("View Post in a new tab"…), through the post type's `view_item` label in the REST API's edit context (`rest_prepare_post_type`), so the two buttons are told apart (GitHub issue #921).
* New: Simpler block editor → Kadence blocks first: Document overview → Outline lists Kadence Advanced Text headings (`kadence/advancedheading` with `htmlTag` heading) with core's, nested ones included, in core's markup and wording; clicking one selects the block. Core's outline only lists `core/heading`, so it was empty on Kadence pages. Shown in place of core's only while the page has an Advanced Text heading (`admin/js/seoprostack-editor-tidy.js`) (GitHub issue #921).
* Fix: Simpler block editor → Other plugins' panels start unpinned missed Hreflang Manager's full (non-Lite) version, `dahm-hreflang-manager/hreflang-manager-sidebar`. It is in `UNPINNED`, and settings version 30 unpins it once for people who had it pinned; `UNPIN_VERSIONS` lists the panels each version added, so a panel pinned again after version 29 stays pinned (GitHub issue #921).
* New: Dev tab in the block editor: Post type (Change post type), Share buttons (Social sharing) and Rank Math's Lock Modified Date move from the Post tab's summary to the top of the Dev tab (`SEOProStack_Editor_Dev_Tab::ROWS`, found by class or by label, translated). Fix: the Dev tab no longer scrolls sideways when a meta box (Kadence Simple Share's) is a few pixels too wide (GitHub issue #921).
* Fix: admin Dark mode: Kadence Blocks' and core's image pickers in block settings (`block-editor-media-placeholder`, Kadence's Background Image) were white; Kadence colour swatches had a faint blurred ring and now have a solid one; the header's Document overview button, pressed, is inverted like core's pressed buttons; Rank Math's SEO and Content AI score in the top bar keeps its solid status colour while its panel is open, so the logo stays dark like the score; Compare revisions (`revision.php`) is dark, with muted red and green diff lines (`admin/css/seoprostack-whole-theme.css`) (GitHub issue #921).

= 1.3.77 =
* Fix: Simpler block editor → Other plugins' panels start unpinned did nothing for people who had used the editor: their `core` → `pinnedItems` preference already saved those panels as pinned (`true`), and 1.3.76 only read unset panels as unpinned. Settings version 29 (`SEOProStack_Editor_Tidy::unpin_saved()`) saves each `UNPINNED` panel that is `true` as `false` once, in `core` and in the pre-6.6 `core/edit-post` scope, for every user with `{prefix}persisted_preferences` on the site, and updates `_modified` so the editor takes the stored copy over its local one. Other preferences are kept; pinning a panel again afterwards stays. Only when Simpler block editor and its Top bar choice are on (GitHub issue #919).
* New, on by default: Simpler block editor → Advanced Text for new lines (`editor_tidy_kadence_text`). With Kadence Blocks, in the post editor (`current_screen` base `post`), `kadence_blocks_settings` reads `adv_text_is_default_editor_block` as true while the site has not saved it, and `kadence_blocks_config_blocks` reads `kadence/advancedheading` `htmlTag` as `p` while that switch reads as on and no tag is saved, as Kadence's own switch saves them. Kadence then calls `setDefaultBlockName()`, so Enter and the default block appender make Advanced Text paragraphs. Kadence's editor panel saves every setting it was given, so `pre_update_option_` filters leave out the values read in, unless the site had saved them; a switch saved off in Kadence wins. Not when Advanced Text is in `kt_blocks_unregistered_blocks` (GitHub issue #919).
* New: page caches are purged once after each SEO Pro Stack update (`SEOProStack_Page_Cache`, `includes/class-seoprostack-page-cache.php`). On `wp_loaded`, when `seoprostack_page_cache_purged` holds an older version, LiteSpeed Cache is purged through its `litespeed_purge_all` action and WP-Optimize through its page cache's `purge()` when that cache is on, and `seoprostack_page_cache_purge` fires for other caches. A cache plugin that is active but skipped on that request by Load plugins only where needed stays in the option's `waiting` list and is purged on the next request that loads it. Per site on multisite; removed on uninstall (GitHub issue #919).
* Developers: `scripts/deploy-sites.sh --apply` no longer reports a LiteSpeed Cache purge as failed just after an update: LiteSpeed Cache's purge request to the site's `admin-ajax.php` gets 30 seconds instead of WordPress's 5 (6 of 17 installs warned in the 1.3.76 rollout). From the starter (wpallstars/wp-plugin-starter-template-for-ai-coding#536).

= 1.3.76 =
* New, on by default: Simpler block editor → Top bar (`editor_tidy_top_bar`, all ticked). View in this tab: an eye button (`admin/js/seoprostack-editor-tidy.js`) placed just before core's View link in the post editor's header, opening the same permalink in the same tab; shown once the post is published. Other plugins' panels start unpinned: Kadence Blocks Controls, Content AI and Hreflang Manager (`SEOProStack_Editor_Tidy::UNPINNED`) are read as unpinned in the `core` → `pinnedItems` editor preference while unset (`get_user_metadata` on `{prefix}persisted_preferences`, after the welcome guide filter), so pinning one again saves and stays. The editor saves every preference when one changes; `SEOProStack_Editor_Tidy::save_pins()` (`update_user_metadata`, `add_user_metadata`) leaves out those unpinned values when the panel had none saved, so nothing is stored for people who never chose. No YellowPencil button: its toolbar script is dequeued in the post editor for everyone, with a CSS rule as a fallback (GitHub issue #917).
* New, on by default: Simpler block editor → Kadence blocks first (`editor_tidy_kadence`). In the post editor, core blocks with a Kadence Blocks (or Pro) counterpart (`SEOProStack_Editor_Tidy::KADENCE_BLOCKS`) get `supports.inserter` false through `blocks.registerBlockType`, when the Kadence block is registered, offered in the inserter and not switched off in `kt_blocks_unregistered_blocks`. Not Preferences → Blocks (`hiddenBlockTypes`): core disallows those blocks, which breaks pasting, transforms, duplicates, Word documents in the editor and patterns that use them. The site editor is unchanged (GitHub issue #917).
* Fix: Simpler block editor: welcome guides and fullscreen mode could still open on sites where a plugin registers a script before `init`. Core reads the editor preferences for `wp-preferences` when the script list is first made, which was then before the feature's `get_user_metadata` filter. `SEOProStack_Editor_Tidy::reload_preferences()` replaces that script's `serverData` with the filtered preferences when core read them first (GitHub issue #917).

= 1.3.75 =
* Fix: Load plugins only where needed: on screens that skip Fluent Boards and Fluent Booking (the Dashboard, Posts, Media for editors), their menu entries were missing. `SEOProStack_Plugin_Loader::plugins_sharing_callback()` counted another plugin as bundling a menu callback's code when it had a file at the same path declaring a class of the same short name, ignoring the namespace; Fluent Boards, Fluent Booking, FluentSMTP and FluentAuth each have `app/Hooks/Handlers/AdminMenuHandler.php`, so the saved menu copy listed FluentSMTP as an owner of their pages and `restore_menu()` left them out wherever FluentSMTP loaded. A copy now counts only when it declares the same namespace (or none for global code). `MAP_VERSION` 12 makes sites learn their screens and menu copy again (GitHub issue #913).

= 1.3.72 =
* Change: Editors use the business plugins makes the chosen plugins' settings read-only for the chosen roles (new `SEOProStack_Editor_Settings`, `includes/class-seoprostack-editor-settings.php`, rules per plugin from a check of each plugin's settings screens and save paths). Saves are refused with a 403 and a message to ask an administrator, before the plugin's own handler: REST routes through `rest_pre_dispatch`, AJAX and admin-post actions and forms posted to the settings screens on `wp_loaded` (before WooCommerce saves its settings), and links that act when opened (Rank Math's `reconnect` and OAuth reply). The read-only screens print a kept notice (`sps-keep`), by script so it also shows inside the Fluent plugins' apps at `#/settings`. Screens and routes that show passwords, API keys or licence keys, or that export settings or change a plugin's set-up, are for administrators only, left out of the menu and refused: WooCommerce's Payments, Integration, REST API keys, webhooks, Status and Extensions, and `payment_gateways`, `system_status`, `webhooks` and those settings groups over REST; Rank Math's Status & Tools, setup wizard and registration, Titles & Meta once `facebook_secret` or `maps_api_key` is set, Sitemap once `youtube_api_key` is, its settings-changing abilities (`rank-math/set-*`, `rank-math/fix-site-seo`); Tutor LMS's Settings, Tools and Add-ons with their AJAX actions; and the Fluent plugins' routes that return keys, integrations or licences (GitHub issue #907).
* Fix: Rank Math's Analytics (and its other modules that check capabilities when they load) for the chosen roles. Rank Math sets up its modules on `after_setup_theme`, before SEO Pro Stack's features boot on `init`, so `rank_math_analytics` was not yet given and Analytics left itself out. The capability filter is now added as SEO Pro Stack loads (`SEOProStack_Editor_Access::early()`), reading its settings from the stored option before `init`, as `SEOProStack_Plugin_Fixes::early()` does (GitHub issue #907).

= 1.3.66 =

The server counts logged-in people's lists as they change, starting from the lists they already had (counted once a site). Visitors' lists stay in their browser, which reports posts joining its first named list or leaving its last in one rate-limited request each way, however many posts a change covers; their lists from before 1.3.66 are not counted. SEOProStack_Post_Reactions::tallies( $post_id ) prints "12 Likes · 3 Shortlists", which the script keeps current on cached pages with the server's words (so plural forms follow the site's language), and buttons() takes 'icons' => true for icon-only buttons (the words kept for screen readers, the like total left to tallies()) (GitHub issue #893).

= 1.3.63 =
* New: Like, save and share has named lists. Liked is the default list: Save and Add to shortlist add a post to it (counting the like) and open Save to list, a dialog with Liked ticked and the person's other lists below, to tick or untick, and New list. A new Your lists block, and the Saved panel, show every list as a tab with its count, with select all, Move selected to, Remove selected, New list, Rename and Delete list (not Liked), an empty-list line, and an actions slot other plugins fill (seoprostack_lists_actions, after seoprostack_saved_panel_actions). Up to 20 lists of up to 500 posts, names up to 60 characters. Visitors' lists stay in their browser, with Log in and register links (filterable), and join their account by list ID, then name, when they log in, without counting likes again. Logged-in people's lists are the per-site user option seoprostack_lists; the Shortlist and Saved lists from before become named lists, and their options are kept in step. Pages stay cacheable, with no personal data or nonce in the HTML (GitHub issue #878).

= 1.3.60 =
* Fix: Watermark pictures removes the watermark from a picture when it becomes the site icon, the site logo (classic or block theme) or Kadence's mobile logo, or gets a Customizer context, whichever comes first, the upload or the choice: the kept original is put back and its sizes made again, as Remove watermark does. Mobile logos are never marked. A Customizer crop (site icon or logo) is made from a clean copy of the chosen picture's kept original, at the size the crop box was drawn on, so the cropped copy is clean and the chosen picture stays marked. When no unmarked original was kept, the picture is left as it is, the server log says why and admins see a notice once. It works while Watermark pictures is off too. A site icon imported and set with WP-CLI kept the mark in browser tabs before (GitHub issue #869).

= 1.3.58 =
* New: Like, save and share has a separate 50-post Shortlist, an Add to shortlist block and shortcode, a Saved panel with keyboard tabs for Shortlist, Saved and Liked, and a List control on Saved posts. Browser lists merge after login; pages contain no personal list data. Item/action filters and a JavaScript lists event and API let other plugins extend the panel without adding messaging here (GitHub issue #851).
* Fix: File and account integrity checks no longer report, as critical, three kinds of file that cannot run code: logs in the uploads folder that exit on their first line and hold no other PHP open tag (Code Profiler Pro's log.php, MC4WP's debug-log.php; read in parts, up to 8 MB), "403 Forbidden" index.php stubs that only send a header and exit, and translation caches (name.l10n.php beside name.mo, WordPress 6.5 and later) in plugins from WordPress.org that hold only literal strings and numbers. The same file with any later <? or <?=, any other call, or no .mo beside it is still reported (GitHub issue #853).
* Fix: Known vulnerabilities from Wordfence Intelligence accepts a download only when it holds at least 1,000 records (the feed has over 40,000), counted across every part of the check. A reply of 1 MB or more that closed early, such as {} padded with whitespace, passed before and replaced the last matches with none; now it fails and keeps them. A check started on 1.3.57 reads its copy again from the start (GitHub issue #854).
* Fix: Fluent Forms colours shows placeholders lighter than typed text, so an empty field no longer looks filled in. A placeholder is the input's text colour faded towards the field (about 5.5:1 on Kadence's light palette, 9:1 on its dark one); a multi-select's search box gets the same colour instead of Kadence's 4.02:1 palette 6 (GitHub issue #844).

= 1.3.57 =
* New, off by default: Known vulnerabilities from Wordfence Intelligence (Security). With a free Wordfence Intelligence API key in wp-config.php (SEOPROSTACK_WORDFENCE_API_KEY), once a day the site (the main site, on a network) downloads Wordfence's Scanner feed, sending only the key, and matches it on the server against every installed plugin and theme and the WordPress version. Wordfence often lists premium plugins and malicious releases first, such as Rank Math SEO PRO 3.0.123's embedded malicious code, which WPVulnerability did not. Matches show in the Known vulnerabilities section and as a critical Site Health test, each linked to its Wordfence record with the version that fixes it, with each copyright holder's notice and licence as the feed's licence asks; new matches are emailed once. The feed (about 80 MB) is read a record at a time in parts of at most 20 seconds, using about 6 MB of memory on PHP 7.4. Wordfence allows a key few downloads, so sites on one server sharing a key share one download through a folder named in SEOPROSTACK_WORDFENCE_FEED_DIR, with a lock and a shared pause after HTTP 429. Results do not autoload and are removed on uninstall (GitHub issue #837).

= 1.3.56 =
* New, off by default: File and account integrity checks (Security). Once a day, read-only and in parts of at most 20 seconds, the site (the main site, on a network) compares WordPress's code files with WordPress.org's checksums and looks for extra PHP files in wp-admin and wp-includes; compares plugins from WordPress.org with the checksums WP-CLI's verify-checksums uses; compares other plugins with a record kept when each was installed, updated or first seen; and looks for PHP in the uploads folder (including .cache files) and administrators added in the last 7 days. Each check is a Site Health test, critical with paths or user names when something is found, and shows in wp seoprostack health, which only reads the last results. New findings are emailed once to the administration address. Nothing is changed or deleted; the seoprostack_integrity_ignore filter skips paths known to change. Records do not autoload and are removed on uninstall (GitHub issue #836).
* New in GitHub builds, off by default: Delay automatic updates (Security) holds WordPress automatic plugin and theme updates for 72 hours after first offered (0–336 hours), including security releases. Update canary skips the hold only on local sites; copied settings cannot bypass it on development, staging or production. Plugins and Updates show hold deadlines; manual, WP-CLI and remotely pushed updates are unchanged. First-seen records do not autoload and are removed on uninstall. The WordPress.org build excludes the feature because it alters update routines (GitHub issue #835).
* New: WPVulnerability preset (single sites). Its email goes daily instead of weekly, so a new finding is usually reported a day or so after WPVulnerability sees it rather than up to a week later (no time is guaranteed: its API cache and WP-Cron can delay it), and it hides the server software managed hosts update (Apache, nginx, MariaDB, MySQL, ImageMagick, curl, Memcached, Redis, SQLite), which otherwise filled every daily email. WordPress, plugins, themes and PHP stay checked; untick server components in the Apply preset dialog on a server you run. The notes give the WP-CLI commands for networks (GitHub issue #840).
* Fix: Known vulnerabilities (Security) no longer counts components hidden in WPVulnerability, by its analysis settings or a WPVULNERABILITY_HIDE_* constant, and says when it left some out. It showed, for example, ImageMagick: 35 after the owner hid ImageMagick (GitHub issue #840).

* Fix: Fixes for other plugins adds WordPress's regenerator-runtime dependency to Image Copytrack 1.2.4's registered vendor script, which uses regeneratorRuntime but declares only wp-element and wp-i18n. Add Plugins, View details and other admin screens no longer throw "regeneratorRuntime is not defined" when no other plugin loads the runtime first. No script is enqueued on its own, no plugin is kept loaded, and Copytrack's settings stay unchanged (GitHub issue #829).
* Fix: Fixes for other plugins separates Tutor LMS's admin body classes from the preceding plugin's. Tutor added tutor-lms without a space, giving acf-browser-chrometutor-lms after Secure Custom Fields, so neither plugin's styles matched. Its callback now receives a separating space even after filters at the same priority; Tutor inactive or skipped is left alone (GitHub issue #828).
* Fix: Tutor LMS's course builder (Courses → Add New, or Edit) is dark in Dark mode. It was kept light because its script draws it in fixed colours; those are now recoloured as on Tutor LMS's other screens, covering the Basics, Curriculum and Additional steps, the Update menu and the lesson and quiz dialogs. The Tutor LMS logo and the Gutenberg icon by "Edit with", drawn in black, follow the text colour, and the editor toolbar, which Tutor's own TinyMCE skin forces white, is dark. Light is unchanged (GitHub issue #830).

= 1.3.54 =
* New: Media in cloud storage uploads, removes, restores and serves registered AVIF copies through picture mode, and backfills existing cloud images. Format copies stay local when their source is not in the confirmed upload inventory, including oversized sources (GitHub issue #818).
* Changed: Cloud media uploads and removal HEAD checks use concurrent groups of up to six. The upload cap is filterable and reduced to keep group payloads within the existing memory allowance. Attempted keys are saved before each group, successful peers are confirmed after a failure, and only complete attachments are published. Local-copy work gets the first 12 seconds of each 25-second batch so uploads cannot starve it; HEAD groups respect its deadline (GitHub issue #819).
* New: Media in cloud storage can hand a site to another bucket with adopt (HEAD/size and optional SHA-256), migrate (verified public GET to signed PUT without local restoration), and explicit forget-previous cleanup. Previous destinations and inventories keep removed files readable on pages and in editors, and support byte-identical restore without old credentials. Automatic batches never delete stored media or replace an unadopted inventory. Status shows destination hosts, buckets, prefixes and handover counts, with a numbered README procedure for people and agents (GitHub issue #816).
* Fix: Cloud media cleanup resumes SHA-256 verification from per-file checkpoints, invalidated by changed generations, inventories or destinations, and skips inventories with no objects or removed files. Status uses bounded bulk inventory queries without evicting post-meta caches and skips queue counts when the settings card does not show the queue. Locally served images keep their unencoded srcset metadata, including non-ASCII filenames.
* Fix: Kadence Pro's dark mode switched with a flash. Its switch changes the palette at once, but social icons, buttons, menu links and the switch itself faded from the old colours for 0.2-0.3 s (light icon squares on a dark bar), and a cached page opened light for a visitor who chose dark, then faded once loaded. Fixes for other plugins applies the saved choice (Kadence's cookie, or the system setting when Kadence follows it) as the body opens and turns transitions off for the two frames in which the palette changes; the switch-style toggle still slides (GitHub issue #822).
* Fix: Dark mode in wp-admin covers more screens. SEO Pro Stack's own filters and cards (Free Plugins) are styled with its own screens, and the sps-own-screen body class no longer joins the next plugin's class (Tutor LMS's tutor-lms). Redux option panels (Kadence Simple Social Share) including their footer, Super Speedy and Scalability Pro, FluentCRM's Search contacts menu in the admin bar, Tutor LMS's course status menus (their eye and arrow icons show again, and Draft's box is dark) and its "Need help" empty-state panel are dark. An error inside a dialog, such as "Plugin not found." in View details, is dark too (GitHub issue #824).

= 1.3.53 =
* New, off by default: Remove local copies after upload (Media in cloud storage's options) deletes an uploaded picture's local files once the bucket confirms each one: only files its record lists for the current connection, all still the uploaded files (size and SHA-256, checked before any goes), with the API answering HTTP 200 and the recorded size, and the public address serving objects. Watermark pictures' unmarked originals, AVIF copies and oversized files stay. Removed pictures show from the bucket on pages, in wp-admin and in the block and classic editors, while saved content keeps local addresses; after a connection change, pages keep the address they were removed under. Files come back, byte for byte, with wp seoprostack media-storage restore [--attachment=<id>] [--all], and just in time for the image editor, size regeneration (wp media regenerate, Regenerate Thumbnails, core's REST steps), WebP and AVIF conversion and watermarking; reading media details never downloads them. Switching removal or the feature off brings every file back in the background, while those pictures keep showing from the bucket; a picture that cannot come back on its own (for example, missing from the bucket) shows its error with its attachment ID and the batch moves on, while only service failures pause it. README gives an optional missing-file rule for after deactivation (GitHub issue #809).
* New, off by default: Kadence query filters use term slugs in addresses (Content tab, under Kadence query filters accept term slugs). Kadence Blocks Pro's taxonomy filter options get term slugs as values, so Kadence's script puts ?make=audi in the address and selects the option a slug names. Around Kadence's kadence_query/query REST route, which draws a loop's results and filters on page load and on every change, slugs in taxonomy filter arguments become term IDs in $_GET (put back afterwards), so results, counts, hidden empty options, page numbers, sorting and search match the ID behaviour, with no redirect. ID links still work; a term whose slug is only digits keeps its ID; other filters are left alone. Replaces the 302 redirect while on (GitHub issue #803).
* New: HTTPS check, on the Security tab and in Tools → Site Health, connects to the site's own address from the server, as a visitor does (behind Cloudflare, that is Cloudflare's certificate and settings), and shows whether the certificate is trusted, matches the host and how many days it has left (a warning under 14), the best TLS version and cipher, whether TLS 1.0 and 1.1 are refused (or that the server cannot tell) and whether the home page sends HSTS for six months or more. Check now runs it again; a link opens the full report and grade on SSL Labs in a new tab. It runs in Site Health and on Check now only, never on a visitor's page, keeps the last result (not autoloaded) and sends nothing to SSL Labs. Replaces Really Simple Security's SSL Labs status box (GitHub issue #804).
* Changed: more of Admin, Security and Plugins is on by default on new installs (GitHub issue #806): HTTPS and security headers, Turn off unused remote access (log and backup files, PHP files in the uploads folder and usernames; XML-RPC and application passwords stay on), Hide the admin bar, Block dashboard access, Hide dashboard widgets, Disable sidebar widgets, Tidy the login screen, Avatars without Gravatar, Plugin presets and Plugins menu in the admin bar. Sites already set up keep their saved settings. Watermark pictures, and features that need a choice first, stay off.
* Changed: Internal linking tools replaces Link Whisper Premium and Link Whisper Free in part (GitHub issue #807). Their Plugins screen notice names what the retirement checks find Link Whisper still does on the site, such as autolinking rules, related posts or link attribute rules, instead of saying it can go; insertion histories and click data are not named. The tools wait while Link Whisper is active; the read-only retirement checks stay available.
* Fix: wp seoprostack convert-images --cleanup-compressx no longer refuses forever because of pictures skipped during conversion. A picture whose file was missing, or an attachment that is not a picture, is now recorded as skipped, not failed; one whose file is back is converted again (by the cleanup itself, or by the next background batch). When the cleanup cannot run it says why: CompressX still active, the feature off, a batch running, how many pictures are waiting, or the IDs of attachments whose copies failed; --dry-run says so too, without changes (GitHub issue #787).
* Fix: Fluent Forms colours uses the input's stronger text colour for placeholders, including the fallback, instead of a low-contrast blend (GitHub issue #801).
* Fix: Copies at their own addresses also covers images in rendered blocks outside post content, including cover block images, block theme template parts and hook-placed Kadence Elements, through render_block. Existing <picture> tags are left alone; pictures set in CSS stay in their original format (GitHub issue #792).
* Fix: Kadence colour check ignores empty and whitespace-only source names returned by the seoprostack_kadence_custom_css filter, so blank names do not appear in its custom CSS warning (GitHub issue #799).

= 1.3.52 =
* Fix: Copies at their own addresses (WebP and AVIF images) hides each <source> in the <picture> tag. Browsers give <source> no display: none of their own, so in the display: contents picture each one became an item of a flex or grid parent and took its gap, moving the picture: a Kadence header logo with light and dark versions moved 80px. The browser still picks among the sources (GitHub issue #789).

= 1.3.51 =
* New: Kadence colour check says when the site's own CSS can change the colours it reports, since it reads only Kadence's settings. When it lists something on a site with rules in Additional CSS or a child theme's style.css, or YellowPencil with saved styles, Site Health names them and says to look at the page in light and dark mode before changing a colour. Plugins can name other sources with the seoprostack_kadence_custom_css filter, an array of names (GitHub issue #785).

= 1.3.50 =
* New: Copies at their own addresses (WebP and AVIF images, off by default) wraps pictures in post and page content, block widgets and attachment images (featured images, logos) in a <picture> tag with AVIF and WebP sources, for pictures in the uploads folder that have copies. The uploads rules then only add the copies' types, without the Accept rewrite, Vary: Accept or Cache-Control: private. Every address always sends one format, so CDNs that ignore Vary, such as Cloudflare's free plan, can cache every picture, and Keep pictures out of shared caches is not used. On multisite the shared rules keep choosing by Accept until every site using the feature has switched it on. Pictures set in CSS stay in their original format (GitHub issue #779).
* Fix: Kadence colour check follows the element's style, as Kadence's CSS does. Social icons in the outline style (no background of their own) and brand-coloured ones (always, or the state "until hover" or "on hover" covers) are not checked. With the pill category style, the category colour is checked as the background behind Kadence's white text, with no fix offered for the white (GitHub issue #781).

= 1.3.49 =
* New, on by default: Fluent Forms colours (Content) makes Fluent Forms labels, help text, inputs, placeholders and file-upload areas follow Kadence's light and dark palette, keeping focused colours. Fluent Forms' saved styles stay unchanged; nothing changes without Fluent Forms and Kadence (GitHub issue #771).
* New: wp seoprostack convert-images --cleanup-compressx (with a read-only --dry-run) deletes CompressX's leftover files and rules after conversion to WebP and AVIF, keeping adopted sidecar images and unrelated rules; the image settings show the leftovers' count and size. It refuses while CompressX is active, a conversion is queued, running, failed or interrupted, or the leftovers include links or special files, and checks every site on multisite (GitHub issue #769).
* Fix: Image loading now loads the first content image from a secondary-query block, such as Kadence Posts or a Query Loop, straight away, with high fetch priority for the first image large enough to be the largest paint; core only did this for the main loop (GitHub issue #768).
* New, on by default: WP Grid Builder colours (Content) makes card surfaces, scheme text and facet inputs follow Kadence's body-scoped dark palette. Light mode and saved WP Grid Builder settings stay unchanged. Only loads with active WP Grid Builder and Kadence, including child themes, where WP Grid Builder prints its styles (GitHub issue #767).
* New, off by default: Kadence query filters accept term slugs (Content tab). Kadence Blocks Pro's taxonomy filters use term IDs in the address, so a link naming the term by its slug (?make=bentley) showed no results; it now goes on (302) to the same address with the term's ID. The argument is the filter's slug or hash; IDs, unknown slugs and other arguments are left alone. Runs on template_redirect at priority 20, after the site's own handlers, and only on pages whose content has a query loop (GitHub issue #776).
* New, on by default: Kadence colour check (Content). A Site Health check lists Kadence Customizer text and background colours where one is a palette colour and the other a fixed one, and the pair is under WCAG AA contrast (4.5:1, 3:1 for buttons) in the light palette or Kadence Pro's dark palette, with both values. It offers to switch the fixed colour to the nearest palette colour (1 to 9) that passes in both modes with every colour paired with it, per colour or all at once, after keeping the old values; Restore puts them back. Nothing changes until someone with Customizer rights clicks, and nothing shows without the Kadence theme (GitHub issue #770).

= 1.3.48 =
* Security: Profile pictures refuse uploads over 5 MB (or the site's lower upload limit), 25 megapixels or 8,000 pixels on either side before decoding. Pictures use GD when it supports their type, falling back to WordPress's image editor otherwise (GitHub issue #761).
* New: wp seoprostack health reads SEO Pro Stack's own Site Health results and web-request records for the site --url names, with table or JSON output, plain-text descriptions/actions, UTC times and --fail-on=critical|recommended. It skips migrations and pending network saving work, never refreshes web probes and saves nothing. Hosting needs keeps an hourly non-autoloaded OPcache snapshot without script paths, and timestamps new memory peaks; CLI results use those web readings, not the command line's PHP values (GitHub issue #756).
* Fix: with Kadence Blocks 3.8.0 or later, Kadence Pro's dark mode stayed light and a dark mode logo vanished on the white header: Kadence Blocks' design tokens gave every palette read the active (light) palette's colours. Fixes for other plugins keeps the theme's colours when another palette, such as the dark one, is asked for; the light palette keeps the token colours (GitHub issue #763).

= 1.3.44 =
* New: a Security tab. HTTPS and security headers and Turn off unused remote access moved there from Admin; their settings are unchanged (GitHub issue #733).
* New, on by default: Minimum password length, on the Security tab. Every new password must be at least 12 characters (8–64), on the profile and reset screens, WooCommerce's account forms and the REST API; passwords already set keep working until changed. Length only, no rules about symbols or capitals (GitHub issue #733).
* New: Known vulnerabilities, on the Security tab, shows how many of WordPress, the plugins, the themes and the server's software WPVulnerability found with known vulnerabilities, with links to Site Health and its settings, or a button to install or activate it. Free Plugins → Admin recommends WPVulnerability. Really Simple Security's notice on the Plugins screen names both replacements (GitHub issue #733).
* Change: Theme, Free Plugins, Pro Plugins, Hosting and Tools are one Recommended tab, with a second row of tabs under the bar. Their addresses are unchanged, so links and bookmarks still work (GitHub issue #733).
* New, on by default (GitHub builds): Auto-update plugins from GitHub, under Updates from GitHub (Maintenance tab). WordPress auto-updates every plugin the shared GitHub updater serves (SEO Pro Stack and any installed plugin that names its GitHub repository, including other authors'), whatever its own toggle on the Plugins screen says, so a plugin whose toggle was never turned on no longer falls behind. Sites updated from an earlier version start auto-updating these plugins after this update. Other plugins keep their own choice, and the auto_update_plugins option is never written: switching it off brings each plugin's toggle back as it was. WordPress's own off switches still win (GitHub issue #726).
* Fix: the Plugins screen warns, before Really Simple Security is deleted, when its Disable "anyone can register" is on while Settings → General → Anyone can register is still ticked underneath: that switch only hides WordPress's setting, so registration opened again once the plugin was gone. SEO Pro Stack leaves the setting for the site owner to untick. Single sites only; multisite registration follows the network's setting (GitHub issue #727).
* Fix: on multisite, Make every site the same no longer loses a site's settings from before when that site applies the network's changes at the same moment (an admin, cron or WP-CLI request on it between SEOProStack_Network_Sites::sync() reading and recording its seoprostack_network_seen). The site then saw no change and skipped its saving work (schedules, rules, files) for the copied settings until they next changed. Now it does that work on its next admin, cron or WP-CLI request (GitHub issue #731).
* Developers: core files synced from WP Plugin Starter 2d8ea29 (1.0.36 and later): SEOProStack_Settings::replace_site_values() keeps a site's settings from before under its save lock, and SEOProStack_Network_Sites::sync() reads and records seoprostack_network_seen under the same lock, then does the saving work after releasing it; visitor requests still never take the lock. Also: tabs with the same 'menu' share a second row of tabs, scripts/sync-core.sh classes each differing file and never undoes a newer starter change, the new scripts/preview-browser.sh signs a browser in to the preview site and reads new debug log entries, and CI checks the ShellCheck runner's architecture only when it has to install ShellCheck (GitHub issues #731, #723).
* Fix: Load plugins only where needed keeps loading, in the post editor, plugins that add to it only through scripts or styles they enqueue on admin_enqueue_scripts, such as YellowPencil, whose pencil button was missing from the editor's toolbar once the editor was learned. The learned plugin requirements are the same for every user, regardless of who opens the editor while it learns. The Dev tab separately hides that button from people who are not developers, without changing which plugins the editor needs. The post editor may load a few more plugins than before (GitHub issue #706).

= 1.3.43 =
* Fix: in Dark mode, the screen shown while the next admin screen loads (Preload pages) was light after a click on a screen that keeps its own look, such as SEO Pro Stats, flashing the content area white until the next screen arrived; it now takes the dark canvas there too. Rank Math's Status & Tools: System Status's rows, and the bars shown while they load, and Database Tools' rows are dark (GitHub issue #725).

* Change: Organise the admin menu puts FluentAuth in the Administrators menu (GitHub issue #728).
* Fix: Tools → Site Health finds files and rsssl_* scheduled tasks left by Really Simple Security after deactivation, including on multisite. Explicit cleanup checks every site's activation lists, clears its marked rules and tasks, and keeps its headers stub, HTTPS redirect, wp-config.php and plugin settings (GitHub issue #719).
* Developers: core files synced from WP Plugin Starter 0c05983, including shared header Light / Dark / System controls and GitHub updater 1.6.2. The existing whole-admin colour mode keeps its own files and controls without colliding with the shared header; uninstall preserves the shared preference while another wpallstars plugin is installed (GitHub issue #722).
* Fix: Turn off unused remote access restores the log and backup files and PHP in uploads .htaccess blocks when another plugin removes them or leaves their markers without the complete rules, on the next main-site admin screen (GitHub issue #718).

= 1.3.41 =
* New: the browser title names the settings tab shown, including instant tab switches and Back, and the view shown in Tools → Links (GitHub issue #707).
* New, off by default: HTTPS and security headers (Admin tab) replaces Really Simple Security (free and Pro), with its login protection left to FluentAuth: http addresses go to https with one 301 (behind Cloudflare and other proxies too), and HTTPS responses get HSTS (this site, or with subdomains ready for preload), nosniff, same-site frames, an HTTPS upgrade for http pictures and scripts, a referrer policy and an opener policy, without X-Powered-By; each part but HSTS can be left out to find a problem. Turn off unused remote access adds Block PHP files in the uploads folder and Hide usernames from visitors, and Tidy the login screen adds Same message for a wrong username, email address or password. Settings version 27 imports Really Simple Security's matching switches, and Hostinger Tools' HTTPS redirect, once. Free Plugins and Pro Plugins no longer list Really Simple Security, and its redirect preset is removed (GitHub issue #698).
* New, on by default: Dev tab in the block editor (Content tab). Panels only the people who build the site need, LiteSpeed Cache's LiteSpeed, Complianz's Cookiebanner, Readabler, Kadence Simple Share's Simple Share Control and Webmention's Webmentions, leave the post editor's Post tab for a Dev tab after Block, in A–Z order, that only developers see (Developer admins: the ticked administrators, or every administrator while that is off, on single sites; super admins, and while it is on the site's ticked administrators, on multisite). Everyone else sees neither, nor YellowPencil's pencil button in the editor's toolbar. The panels' fields still save with the post. The seoprostack_editor_dev_panels filter adds or removes panels (GitHub issue #704).
* Change: More menu in the admin bar is text only. Plugins' icons differed in size and some items had none, so names did not line up; they are hidden in the menu, and items that showed only an icon (such as LiteSpeed Cache) start with their plugin's name.
* New: Multisite network settings. Network Admin → Settings → SEO Pro Stack shows the same header (marked Network), search, tabs and cards as each site's screen, for super admins, and saves the network's own settings, kept apart from each site's. New sites, and sites where SEO Pro Stack runs for the first time, start with them. Apply to every site, under each card, makes every site use the card's network settings: each site's screen shows the card locked, saying the network administrator sets it (with a link to the network screen for super admins), and Recommended speed settings offers no Turn on or Undo for it. Switched off, each site has its own settings again, as they were never overwritten. Each card names the sites whose own settings differ (up to 10, with links to their settings screens), with Make every site the same, which copies the card's network settings into every site's own settings once; networks of more than 500 sites are not compared, nor offered Make every site the same. When the network changes a site's settings, the site runs its saving work (schedules, rules and files, and Load plugins only where needed learns its pages again) on its next admin, cron or WP-CLI request, never on a visitor's page; the must-use file of Load plugins only where needed and the fixes that run before plugins load read the applied settings too. Settings that only make sense on each site stay there: the Developers list, the roles and page of Hide the admin bar and Block dashboard access, 410 Gone's removed addresses, Delayed Google Analytics' measurement ID, Website screenshots' service key, Watermark pictures' picture, the plugin lists of Load plugins only where needed, and Custom fields to content (GitHub issue #694).
* Change: on multisite, super admins are developers on every site, and each site's own administrators can be developers too. Developer admins' Developers list, before on single sites only, is on every site, with super admins ticked and locked so the site's developers cannot untick them; switching the feature on ticks you there, as on single sites. When the network switches Developer admins on for every site, each site's developers are its super admins and the administrators already ticked there (GitHub issue #694).
* New: on the network screen, the Plugins tab has Clean up every site now (Clean up deleted plugins), which returns there with its notice; Turn off unused remote access, WebP and AVIF images and Load plugins only where needed say in their options how the main site's files serve every site; and Pro Plugins, Hosting and Tools are there too, with a Free version network active badge. In the network admin, the admin bar's star opens the network screen; on each site, it has a Network settings link for super admins (GitHub issue #694).
* Change: networks no longer show Load large settings only where they are used, which does nothing there, and Hosting needs names a site's largest settings without suggesting it. On single sites, Hosting needs' suggestion names the Server tab, where that setting is, instead of the Speed tab (GitHub issue #694).
* Fix: Clean up deleted plugins named a deleted plugin "0" when WordPress's Recently active list held the empty entry it leaves after a deactivation when the list was empty (0 => false). The entry is still removed, without being named (GitHub issue #694).
* Fix: on phones, text fields on the settings screen fit their card instead of running past its edge (GitHub issue #694).
* Developers: core files synced from WP Plugin Starter (80cc6d9, and its issue #457, which keeps them within PHP Mess Detector's size limits): the network settings and screen (includes/class-seoprostack-network-settings.php, includes/class-seoprostack-network-sites.php, includes/class-seoprostack-network-differences.php and admin/includes/class-network-manager.php); settings take 'network' => false to stay with each site, and multi settings 'locked' (always chosen, never stored); admin tabs take 'network' => true to show on the network screen; new actions seoprostack_settings_changed (settings changed in any way, including by the network for this site; use it rather than update_option_seoprostack_options), seoprostack_network_setting_panel and seoprostack_network_settings_tab_after; seoprostack_setting_saved also fires when the network changes a site's settings, while SEOProStack_Network_Sites::syncing() is true. CI's ShellCheck also runs on ARM64 runners, and the README badge scripts refuse malformed markers. Features that worked on update_option_seoprostack_options use seoprostack_settings_changed, and SEOProStack_Plugin_Loader::options() gives code that runs before plugins load the applied settings. Uninstall deletes the seoprostack_network network option, its save lock (seoprostack_network_lock, on the main site) and each site's seoprostack_network_seen. STANDARDS.md → Multisite has the rules features follow. Single sites: no change; they never read the network option (GitHub issue #694).

= 1.3.40 =
* New: Calls to other sites (Server tab) replaces HTTP Requests Manager, whose latest release (1.3.11) has an unauthenticated stored XSS with no fix (CVE-2026-39724). Slow calls are marked (someone waited 3 seconds or more for one, or 1 second on average), and each plugin and site gets a choice: Allow, Limit to 3 seconds (while someone waits; not cron, WP-CLI or downloads), Block on visitor pages or Block everywhere, with who chose it and when. The last 50 changes are listed under Choices made. Blocks made before keep blocking everywhere. Hosting needs' slow-page advice leaves out blocked calls. Free Plugins no longer lists HTTP Requests Manager and its preset is removed; the Plugins screen suggests deactivating and deleting it, naming its Smart block limits and rules while they are on. New seoprostack_outbound_history option (not autoloaded), removed on uninstall (GitHub issue #693).
* Fix: Calls to other sites said pages do not wait for calls that do not read the answer ('blocking' => false, as when WordPress starts cron), and Hosting needs left them out of its slow-page advice. With cURL, as on most hosts, WordPress still waits for them, up to their time limit: on a test site, such a call to a site that answered after 6 seconds took 6 seconds. They now count as waits, can be limited, and show Answer not read (GitHub issue #693).
* Fix: on narrow screens, Tools → Calls to other sites scrolled sideways as a whole (773 pixels wide on a 375-pixel phone); now only its table scrolls (GitHub issue #693).

= 1.3.39 =
* New, on by default: Quiet Imagify suggestions (Admin tab). Rank Math Pro, WP Rocket and BackWPup no longer show "… recommends optimizing your images … using Imagify" with an Install Imagify Plugin button in the Media Library, the editor and Rank Math's settings, since WebP and AVIF images and Resize large uploads do that job. It uses WP Media's own filter and reads its dismissal option as set without saving it; switch it off to get the suggestion back (GitHub issue #700).
* Fix: with Organise the admin menu, plugins' scripts that look for their own menu by WordPress's markup (such as Complianz, on every screen and tab) find it again for the menus in Administrators and Developers, so their screens no longer stop with a JavaScript error, and the current entry in the menu's third level follows the plugin's tabs. The menu looks and works as before (GitHub issue #692).
* Fix: with Organise the admin menu and GOTMLS Anti-Malware both active, every admin page no longer adds a "map_meta_cap was called incorrectly" notice about read_post to the debug log (GitHub issue #696).

= 1.3.38 =
* Change: Search custom fields checks only the searched fields’ current meta names before matching values, including group, repeater and flexible content rows, while keeping the field reference check. Values under a field’s former name are no longer searched; clone and unsupported containers keep the previous search (GitHub issue #689).
* New: Search custom fields can leave fields out. A search never shows a matched value, but a match tells which post holds it, so fields your pages never show should not be searched. Plugins that define fields leave theirs out with the new seoprostack_field_search_field filter, which also says whether the search is on the site or in an admin list; site owners list field names or keys in Fields to leave out, under Troubleshooting. Leaving out a group, repeater or flexible content field leaves out everything in it. With neither, searches are unchanged (GitHub issue #688).

Confirmed uploads are served from the public bucket while local files and saved addresses stay. Constants-only configuration, per-site switches, connection test, progress and WP-CLI commands (GitHub issue #684).

= 1.3.34 =
* Removed: the Modern admin colours setting. Pick a scheme in your profile (Users → Profile → Admin Color Scheme) instead; from WordPress 7.0, Modern is WordPress's own default ("Default" in the list). After the update everyone sees the scheme saved in their profile again. On sites where the setting was on and WordPress is older than 7.0, people who never picked a scheme keep Modern: it is saved in their profile once, during the update, in place of none or of Fresh, which WordPress saves for every new user (WordPress 7.0 makes the same change when it updates). Other schemes, and a scheme someone picked while the setting was on, are not changed (settings version 26; GitHub issue #678).

= 1.3.33 =
* New: the colour mode covers all of wp-admin and moves to an icon in the admin bar, just left of your name. A click turns Dark on or off; its menu offers Light, Dark and System. Dark gives every admin screen (Dashboard, lists, Plugins, the block editor's panels, Media, Site Health and SEO Pro Stack's own screen) dark backgrounds while your admin colour scheme, Modern included, keeps its menu, admin bar and accent colour. The choice is shared with SEO Pro Stats (user meta wpallstars_admin_theme, kept on uninstall while SEO Pro Stats is installed), and a change in either plugin shows in both at once. The button in SEO Pro Stack's header is gone. New seoprostack_admin_dark_screen filter keeps a screen light (GitHub issue #655).
* Fix: in dark mode, the settings screen's switches, links, tabs, outlines and logo follow the admin colour scheme, as SEO Pro Stats' screens do. They were WordPress's old default blue in every scheme, Modern included, because the scheme's colour was read before WordPress sets it. Accent text keeps the scheme's hue at a lighter tone that reads at 4.5:1 or more on the dark surfaces, and primary buttons are darkened just enough for their labels. In light mode, lighter schemes' colours, such as Sunrise's orange, are darkened just enough to read at 4.5:1, and the classic schemes' bright link hover is darker. Turning Modern admin colours on or off recolours the screen at once (GitHub issue #670).
* Fix (GitHub builds): Check again on the Updates screen offers a release published since the last check, and a failed check for updates on GitHub is shown under the plugin on the Plugins screen, with a Check again now link, and on the Updates screen, instead of failing silently (shared updater 1.6.1 from WP Plugin Starter 1.0.32).
* Developers: core files synced from WP Plugin Starter (1ae9182): the workflows run on a self-hosted runner only when the repository variable CI_RUNNER names one (not set here, so nothing changes), and CI's lint job uses a pinned, checksum-verified ShellCheck 0.11.0; scripts/readme-badges.sh matches the starter's again (GitHub issue #668).
* Developers: core files synced from WP Plugin Starter 1.0.32: the shared updater above; STANDARDS.md has the rules for every banner and icon picture; repository metrics refresh again (repo-metrics.yml uses aidevops v3.39.1) (GitHub issue #671).
* Developers: DEVELOPMENT.md keeps Qlty among the organization's review apps, and says to merge without its check when the monthly minutes run out and never to make it required, as in WP Plugin Starter (GitHub issue #675).

It hides SEO Pro Stats's matching notice, and WP Crontrol's screen uses the same check (GitHub issue #659).

If you may change SEO Pro Stack's settings, picking it turns Modern admin colours off; for people who cannot, their own choice applies to them until the setting is turned on again (GitHub issue #664).
