= 0.11.1 =
* Developers: the core files (listed in scripts/core-files.txt) now come from the starter plugin, wpallstars/wp-plugin-starter-template-for-ai-coding, with SEO Pro Stack's names: scripts/sync-core.sh copies them and scripts/sync-core.sh --check lists any that differ. SEO Pro Stack's own admin styles and script moved to admin/css/seoprostack-tabs.css and admin/js/seoprostack-tabs.js, loaded from SEOProStack_Setup on the new seoprostack_admin_enqueue action, and use the shared helpers in seoprostackAdmin.api. The presets and starter data release check moved to scripts/preflight-plugin.sh. With no settings at all, the settings screen shows its first tab with "No settings yet." Nothing changes for users.
* Developers: the docs are split so the shared part can be copied between plugins as it is. STANDARDS.md (new) holds the rules every plugin made from the starter shares: structure and core files, code rules, Updates from GitHub, releases, dark mode and testing. DEVELOPMENT.md and RELEASING.md are the same in every plugin, with names as placeholders. AGENTS.md keeps only SEO Pro Stack's own rules (features on by default, replaced plugins, presets, starter data), and LAUNCH.md (new) its WordPress.org checklist, guideline review and public launch state. The checklist's Defaults line now lists Fixes for other plugins, which is on by default too.

= 0.11.0 =
* Change: in Free Plugins, Advanced Database Cleaner, Anti-Malware Security and Brute-Force Firewall, Debug Log Manager, Query Monitor, User Switching and WP Crontrol say they are not needed day to day: deactivate or delete them so the site runs at full speed, and turn them on only to look into a problem or for admin work.
* Change: Updates from GitHub now uses a shared updater that other wpallstars plugins can carry too. Only the newest copy on the site runs, so all of them are checked together, once. Private repositories can use one WPALLSTARS_GITHUB_TOKEN in wp-config.php for every plugin; SEOPROSTACK_GITHUB_TOKEN and the seoprostack_github_* filters still work.
* Change: Free Plugins lists WP-Optimize again on LiteSpeed servers, with a note that the site does not need it there; on other servers its note says it is for its page cache. The WP-Optimize preset turns that page cache on, on servers other than LiteSpeed, through WP-Optimize's own save code, so Reset and Undo turn it off and back on cleanly.
* Change: the WooCommerce preset turns on Deferred emails, so checkout does not wait for the mail server. Emails go out through Action Scheduler, which needs WP-Cron or a server cron. If emails arrive late or not at all, check WooCommerce → Status → Scheduled Actions (group woocommerce-emails) for pending or failed woocommerce_send_queued_transactional_email actions, and turn Deferred emails off in WooCommerce → Settings → Advanced → Features. Reset and Undo still work.
* Fix: WebP and AVIF images now use the quality you set; before, WordPress's own quality was used whatever the setting said. Changing a quality converts the pictures again in the background, and copies from earlier versions are made again once.
* New: WebP and AVIF images: Quality by picture size (on), sharper thumbnails and smaller large pictures.
* Change: WebP and AVIF images makes sharper copies by default: WebP quality 90 and AVIF 70, instead of 80 and 60. Sites that saved their own quality keep it.
* New: WebP and AVIF images checks that browsers get the copies, and says what to do when they do not, such as restarting LiteSpeed so it reads the new rules.
* New: Watermark pictures can mark, mark again or unmark the whole Media Library in the background from its settings, with progress and a Stop button. Bulk actions carry on in the background instead of leaving pictures out. In the Media Library's tile view, a picture's details now have Add or Remove watermark.
* Fix: background picture work no longer runs past the server's time limit when several jobs run together, and no longer stops before it starts on servers that have run for a long time (watermark bulk actions seemed to do nothing).
* New, off by default: Restrict content shows posts, pages, categories and blocks only to logged-in people, logged-out visitors or chosen roles. Choose “Who sees this” in the editor, on a category or tag, or in a block’s Visibility panel. Others see what is above the More block, or the excerpt, and a message with a login link, also in lists, feeds and the REST API. A Members only block keeps part of a public post for members. Shop products can be kept for members too: WooCommerce hides their price and details, and neither WooCommerce nor FluentCart lets others buy them. Membership, payment and sign-up plugins give people a role, and the role decides what they see. Replaces Content Control, now closed on WordPress.org: its block rules and [content_control] shortcode keep working, and its restrictions for chosen posts and categories carry over.

= 0.10.1 =
* Change: Short links no longer counts as a replacement for Lasso Lite (Simple URLs), which also shows product displays and reports. The Plugins screen stops suggesting you remove Lasso Lite, Free Plugins lists it again, and the two run side by side. Short links still imports Lasso Lite's links if you want to move them.
* Change: the Short links list no longer shows other plugins' links under each link, such as AI Engine's Magic Wand and Social Engine's Create Social Post.
* New: LiteSpeed hosting. The LiteSpeed Cache preset is now worked out for each site: CSS and JS minified, the cache purged after updates, the page cache and browser cache on when the site runs on a LiteSpeed server, and LiteSpeed's own JS defer, WebP, Heartbeat and emoji settings off where SEO Pro Stack already does that job. Applying it saves through LiteSpeed Cache, as its own screen does. On LiteSpeed servers, Free Plugins no longer suggests WP-Optimize, and Hosting needs names the server, suggests LiteSpeed Cache and warns when WP-Optimize's page cache or minify runs alongside it.
* Fix: No fade between admin screens also stops a white flash where the toolbar goes at the top of the screen, seen on each screen change on sites with a long admin menu.

= 0.10.0 =
* New, off by default: Clean the database weekly (Speed tab). Once a week, in the background, removes expired temporary data, posts and comments that have been in the bin longer than WordPress keeps them, older spam, old automatic drafts and details left by deleted posts, comments and terms, and can optimise tables with a lot of unused space. Its options show what is waiting, with a Clean now button; wp seoprostack clean-database does the same from the command line. Where LiteSpeed Cache runs on a LiteSpeed server it replaces WP-Optimize, whose cleanup choices are imported.
* Change: the plugin's website link on the Plugins screen goes to its GitHub page. Security reports now have a private route (SECURITY.md).
* Fix: Fit the menu to its names keeps the menu the same width on every screen, instead of moving a few pixels between screens. Once you have seen a screen that needs it wider, every screen uses that width until your plugins change.
* Change: Short links can import Lasso Lite (Simple URLs) links, if you want to move them. They are imported with their /go/ address, target, nofollow and sponsored options, name, click count and groups, when you deactivate Lasso Lite, with Import links in the Short links settings, or with WP-CLI, and keep working the same way. Short links does not replace Lasso Lite, which also has product displays, so both can stay active. Lasso Lite’s own data is never changed.
* Change: the Short links list no longer shows other plugins' links under each link, such as AI Engine's Magic Wand and Social Engine's Create Social Post.
* Change: a clearer description of what SEO Pro Stack does, with features grouped by what they help with, and a note that it is built with aidevops, an open-source AI harness.
* Fix: Like, save and share buttons no longer take the theme's button colours and drop shadow after a click. New Button colour setting: a colour from the Kadence theme's palette, including its Notices colours, so the buttons follow dark mode. Also in Customize → Colors & Fonts → Colors.
* New, off by default: Brand icons: a Brand icon block with about 3,800 brand logos from the current Simple Icons set, plus Font Awesome for brands Simple Icons no longer has (such as LinkedIn, Microsoft and Slack), in the brand's colour or your text colour, optionally linked. Icons are part of the page, so nothing extra loads. Replaces Popular Brand Icons – Simple Icons, whose [simple_icon] shortcode and #name# menu titles keep working.
* New: with Brand icons on, Kadence Blocks' icon pickers have a Brand icons category, so any Kadence block with an icon can show a brand logo.
* New, on by default with Organise the admin menu: Writers see only writing. Contributors and authors see their posts, media, comments and profile, and no longer see or open plugin pages that any writer could reach, such as plugin settings or short links, or Tools. Pages a plugin gives their role on purpose stay.
* New: Buy me a coffee, at the top right of Settings → SEO Pro Stack, opens the maker’s Buy Me a Coffee page, if you would like to support more open-source work.
* Change: Hide admin notices shows a megaphone instead of a bell in the admin bar, so it is not mistaken for other plugins’ notification bells. Debug Log Manager’s icon now always sits just left of it. The dot sits at the megaphone’s top right, and turns red while at least one notice in the panel can be dismissed.
* Change: the Read Me says how many plugins SEO Pro Stack replaces, and how their downloads compare in size with SEO Pro Stack's.
* Change: Free Plugins’ All list shows how much disk space each installed plugin takes, split into PHP, JavaScript, CSS, media and other files, as on the Plugins screen. Plugin icons line up with the middle of each row. The Status column is gone: the Install, Activate or Deactivate button shows it, and Go Pro buttons have a column of their own.
* New, off by default: Like, save and share: Like, Save and Share buttons after posts (or anywhere with a block), and a Saved posts block listing what each visitor saved. Works with page caching and sets no cookies: visitors' likes and saved posts stay in their browser, logged-in people's in their account. Replaces Favorites: its like counts, logged-in users' favourites, post types and shortcodes carry over.
* Change: Sticky posts for any post type is now Pinned posts for any post type. Post lists show a pin instead of a star, and lists, Quick Edit, Bulk Edit and the editors say “Pinned” instead of “Sticky”. Clicking the pin adds or removes “Pinned” after the title straight away. The pin and Order by hand columns are narrower, leaving more room for the title.
* Change: Load plugins only where needed loads fewer plugins on post lists such as Pages. Plugins that only add Quick Edit or Bulk Edit fields to other lists (WooCommerce for products) are skipped there, and general plugins that say they work with WooCommerce no longer bring WooCommerce to every screen. Screens are learned again once.
* Fix: with Fit the menu to its names, the block editor in full screen mode no longer leaves an empty strip where the wider menu would be, and the menu is fitted when the editor leaves full screen instead of wrapping names at the usual width.
* New, off by default: Word documents in the editor: drop or paste a Word document (.docx) into the block editor, or choose one from the Options menu, and it becomes blocks, with headings, formatting, links, lists, quotes, tables and pictures, which are added to your Media Library (replaces Mammoth .docx converter).
* Fix: More menu in the admin bar: an item no longer shifts to the right when the item above it has a taller icon (Rank Math SEO under AutomatorWP, Query Monitor under Social Posts).

= 0.9.0 =
* Change: the Advise.so card on the Tools tab now describes the Advise.so SEO community, where you can chat to the maker of SEO Pro Stack, with buttons to join the community and visit its home page.
* New, off by default: an Agency tab for selling services and apps with Fluent Forms, FluentCRM, Fluent Boards, Fluent Support, FluentBooking, FluentCommunity and Tutor LMS. Order flow: when an order form is paid, the order goes on your board, a support conversation opens for it, and the client can get a login, a course and a community space; when you move the order to another stage, the client hears about it by email. Quote and referral forms go on a sales board, and support requests open a conversation. A Client dashboard block shows each logged-in client their orders and how far along they are, their messages, report, upcoming calls, payments, courses and community, and nothing of anyone else's. Example data for a digital marketing agency (order forms for SEO, local SEO, websites, directory listings, ads, PR, social media, design, apps and retainers, quote, support, referral and brief forms, boards, support products, Discovery Call and Monthly Review Call, a client course and space, and draft pages) can be added and removed from the Agency tab; adding it also switches on Order flow and Client dashboard for those examples. Nothing to choose: the tab has just the two switches, the example data sets everything up, and a copied order form named “… Order Form” works for a new service with nothing to set. Every order form is a two-column checkout: the choices first, then the service and its questions, in two thirds, and Your order (running total, discount code and button) in one third, below the form on phones. Link Building shows it with free Fluent Forms (link packs with a quantity) and Local SEO with Fluent Forms Pro (adds discount codes, bank transfer and photo uploads). Example choices show their prices. With Order flow on, a choice field's Layout in the Fluent Forms editor also offers buttons in one row or in 1 to 4 columns, with wrapping text and Fluent Forms' colours; in a field that is not required the chosen button can be clicked again to clear it, and choosing a subscription plan clears one chosen in another field, as Fluent Forms takes one per order.
* New: starter data can now add Fluent Support products, FluentCommunity spaces, Tutor LMS courses, draft pages and SEO Pro Stack settings, and keeps anything in use when removed. Fluent Booking events can say how the call takes place and their colour.
* New, off by default: Link cards adds a Link card block: paste an address to show the page's title, description, site and picture, with the picture copied to your Media Library so visitors never contact the other site (replaces Bookmark Card, whose cards keep showing and convert in one click). Wikipedia previews shows a short preview of the article when visitors hover over, focus or tap a link to Wikipedia (replaces Wikipedia Preview, whose marked words keep working).
* New, off by default: Search custom fields makes site and admin searches look in Advanced Custom Fields and Secure Custom Fields text fields, repeater rows included (replaces ACF: Better Search). Old post addresses clears old post addresses that can no longer lead anywhere and lists the rest under Tools → Old addresses with when each was last used (replaces Slugs Manager: Delete Old Permalinks, closed on WordPress.org).
* Change: Order by hand: while a list is sorted by a column, a Custom order link takes you back to the order you can drag. Searches and the Drafts and Pending views show the hand order too.
* Change: Deactivate links on SEO Pro Stack’s settings come back to the same page.
* New: the Plugins screen notes, under each plugin SEO Pro Stack can replace, which setting makes it redundant and what to do next.
* New, on by default: Fixes for other plugins (Plugins tab) works around other plugins’ bugs without changing their settings. Lasso Lite (Simple URLs) no longer contacts lasso.link on every admin screen.
* Change: Free Plugins no longer suggests Disable All WordPress Updates: the Speed and Plugins features cover why it was used, and it also blocks updates from GitHub.
* New, off by default: Menu item visibility shows a menu item only to logged-in people, logged-out visitors or chosen roles (replaces Nav Menu Roles and reads its rules). Change post type turns a post into a page or another type from the editor, Quick Edit or Bulk Edit (replaces Post Type Switcher). Order by hand lets you drag posts, pages and categories into order (replaces Simple Custom Post Order and imports its order). Term tools merges terms, moves them to another taxonomy or sets their parent, and redirects their old addresses (replaces Term Management Tools).
* Change: block editors open faster with Simpler block editor on: the site details they ask for are no longer built with a description of every plugin’s REST routes. Load plugins only where needed also puts its menu entries back with fewer permission checks.
* New: Report a problem, at the top right of Settings → SEO Pro Stack, opens the plugin’s GitHub issues so you can tell us what went wrong.
* Change: Load plugins only where needed learns the Customizer, Import and Export forms; their contributors stay loaded. Saving, previews, imports and downloads still load every plugin. Missing Customizer widgets, areas or menu locations reload before the form opens. Site Health stays fully loaded for accurate diagnostics.
* Change: Faster editor with Kadence Blocks keeps Kadence’s design library in your browser until Kadence updates it, and draws only the patterns on screen, so in a new editor page the library opens in about 2 seconds instead of 6. Sync still fetches new designs; licence and account data are never kept.
* New: a banner at the top of the Read Me tab, and for the WordPress.org plugin page: “SEO Pro Stack: WP Admin – Solved!”

* Fix: Organise the admin menu: Code Snippets shows its logo in the Administrators and Developers menus.

= 0.8.0 =
* New, off by default: Maintenance mode (Maintenance tab): a plain-text 503 page with Retry-After and noindex, an administrator reminder, and revocable 24-hour bypass links. Imports Hostinger Tools' maintenance switch without changing its settings; its other enabled jobs are named on the Plugins screen.
* New: display presets for Rank Math SEO (no Frontend Stats Bar), WP-Optimize (no caching menu in the admin bar) and Burst Statistics (no non-critical dashboard notices), leaving modules and schedules alone. Burst's tracking settings and generated script stay unchanged through Apply, Reset and Undo.
* New: Code Snippets preset hides upgrade notices and its admin bar menu. Apply, Reset and Undo clear the preset's named caches, including Code Snippets' versioned settings cache on Redis and other persistent caches.
* New: Fluent Booking starter data: a free, 30-minute Discovery Call for the administrator adding it. Confirmed bookings join Website Booking Form with FluentCRM active. Existing calendars stay unchanged; Remove keeps events with bookings and calendars in use.
* New, off by default: Turn off unused remote access, with two unchecked choices: XML-RPC (403 response, no pingback header or RSD link) and application passwords (no profile section or Authorize application screen). Imports enabled switches from active Hostinger Tools and Disable Bloat once, without overwriting saved choices or changing their options. Other Hostinger tools are not replaced.
* New, off by default: Database key cleanup. Review leftover and duplicate indexes under Tools → Database keys, confirm each removal, and keep a restore statement in the log. Core and known active-plugin keys are protected. Back up first.
* New, off by default: learn which plugins each public page needs, with an opt-out list. Plain pages can skip WooCommerce PHP with Lighter WooCommerce pages on; shop pages and cart displays keep it. Sessions and unknown pages load every plugin.
* New, off by default: TranslatePress switcher colours (Content tab) follows Kadence light and dark modes for floating and shortcode switchers, with menu labels following the theme. Turning it off restores TranslatePress's own colours; its settings are never changed.
* New: the Read Me tab thanks the makers of the plugins SEO Pro Stack replaces or learned from (Credits), with links to each one. Its tables now show as tables, and links to other sections jump to them.
* New, off by default: six features that do most of Disable Bloat’s job. Tidy admin screens (no “Thank you for creating with WordPress” footer, no update notice for people who cannot update, optionally no file editors), Tidy WooCommerce admin (no marketplace suggestions, Extensions menu, WooCommerce.com connect notices or app promotion in order emails), Tidy the login screen (logo links to your site), Simpler block editor (no welcome guides or block directory, and optionally no core patterns, template editor or fullscreen mode), Remove WordPress extras (no emoji script, version number, old blogging app links or shortlinks) and Lighter WooCommerce pages (WooCommerce’s scripts only on shop pages and pages with WooCommerce blocks). While Disable Bloat is active, its settings are imported once and the features wait until you deactivate it.
* New, off by default: Fewer Heartbeat requests (WordPress’s once-a-minute check slowed to once an hour on admin screens and left out on the site; the editors keep it for post locks and autosave) and Limit post revisions (keep the newest 10, or the number you choose). Also offered now: no embed links or password strength meter (Remove WordPress extras), the classic widgets screen (Simpler block editor) and no Marketing → Overview (Tidy WooCommerce admin). Disable Bloat’s matching switches are imported too.
* Change: the Plugins screen says what a plugin SEO Pro Stack replaces still does on your site that SEO Pro Stack does not, such as Disable Bloat’s REST API switches, instead of saying it can go.
* Change: Pro Plugins no longer lists Disable Bloat PRO, Pretty Links Pro or Admin Menu Editor Pro, which SEO Pro Stack replaces.
* New: starter data for Fluent Forms: a Contact Form, Discovery Call Form, Subscription Form and Data Subject Access Request Form, named for what they are for. With FluentCRM active, each sends people to the FluentCRM list named after it and tags them from their answers (the newsletter tick box, or who they are on the access request). Phone fields and proof uploads need Fluent Forms Pro and are left out without it. Remove keeps forms with entries, forms placed on a page and forms changed since.
* Change: Free Plugins and Pro Plugins no longer recommend Freesoul Deactivate Plugins. Load plugins only where needed (Plugins tab) does its job in wp-admin and for the whole site.
* Change: Plugin presets: Apply preset names each setting as the plugin’s own settings screen does, with a line on what it does and its values in plain words (Off, On, Private) instead of stored names and codes.
* Fix: Load plugins only where needed now loads plugins that add columns, filters or row links to a list on that list, so Rank Math’s SEO columns show on the Posts list again. Every screen is checked again once.
* Fix: Load plugins only where needed no longer leaves WP Sheet Editor’s spreadsheets blank: pages of a framework that several plugins bundle load all of those plugins.
* Fix: Ask before licence checks: “Ask me again” now keeps each plugin hidden for the time you chose. Hiding a second plugin, such as Starter Templates after Kadence Blocks, brought back the first.
* Fix: Organise the admin menu: dark plugin icons in the Administrators and Developers menus (Site Assist, ICS Calendar, Bit Social, FluentCRM) now show in the menu’s colours, as at the top level, and AutomatorWP shows a cog instead of nothing on screens that skip it.
* New: Readable list columns removes Complianz’s Website Scan, Post Type Switcher’s Type and Burst Statistics’ Pageviews columns (Columns to remove; Admin Columns can still add them back), puts Author and Date last on content lists, and keeps Rank Math’s SEO Details on one line each.
* New: Organise the admin menu widens the sidebar, up to 280 pixels, so menu names fit on one line (Fit the menu to its names, on by default). Other plugins’ fixed headers, footers and panels laid out for the usual menu width, such as Fluent Support’s setup footer, move over or narrow with it.
* Change: Organise the admin menu shows FluentSMTP’s logo for its entry instead of a cog.
* Fix: More menu in the admin bar: TranslatePress’s Translate Site item now starts at the left like the other items, instead of being spread across the menu.

= 0.7.0 =
* New, off by default: Ask before licence checks (Plugins tab). Premium plugins and themes stop checking their licence with their maker’s server until you choose, per plugin: allow it once now, once a day (the answer is reused for the rest of the day, so pages do not wait) or never. You are asked on the next admin screen, or again tomorrow, in a week, a month or a year, and can choose again or forget your choices from the Licence checks link on the Plugins screen. Update checks are left alone.
* New: Plugin presets: Apply preset opens a window listing each setting it changes, now and after, so you can untick any you want to keep.
* Change: Tidy the dashboard also hides WooCommerce Setup, which repeats the task list on WooCommerce → Home.
* Change: MasterStudy LMS is no longer recommended; Tutor LMS is the recommended LMS. Free Plugins, Pro Plugins, Organise the admin menu and Tidy the dashboard drop their MasterStudy entries.
* New, on by default in copies from GitHub: Updates from GitHub. SEO Pro Stack, and other plugins that name their GitHub repository, now update from GitHub on the Updates screen without the Git Updater plugin, also on PHP 7.4. Auto-updates and View details work as for other plugins. GitHub is asked at most twice a day. While Git Updater is active it keeps doing the job; you can then deactivate and delete it. The prompt to install Git Updater is gone.
* New: the Plugins screen lists plugins SEO Pro Stack can do the job of: deactivate the ones whose setting is on, switch on the setting for the others, and delete the ones no longer needed. Hide hides the list.
* Change: Tidy the dashboard also hides Debug Log Manager’s box, which read the whole debug log on every Dashboard load (6 of 7 seconds on a test site). Tools › Debug Log Manager shows the same entries.
* Fix: copies installed from GitHub: after GitHub answered Git Updater with an error (such as its rate limit), Git Updater asked GitHub again on every page load. Each error is now cached for as long as Git Updater means to.
* New: starter data for FluentCRM and Fluent Boards, on the Plugins screen with Plugin presets on. One click adds the lists, tags and contact fields we organise contacts with (lists say where someone came from, tags who they are to you and on the website), tags by WordPress role, and a Website board for requests with a "please check" step. Only what is missing is added, and Remove takes back what was added while it is unused.
* New: Plugin presets for EventON Lite (its files only on calendar pages, no Google Fonts), FluentCRM (anonymised IP addresses, no Gravatar), Fluent Forms (no weekly summary, IP logging or admin bar menu), Kadence Blocks (Google Fonts from your own site), Lasso Lite and LiteSpeed Cache (no promotions), Tutor LMS (private student profiles, reviews wait for approval, courses in the block editor, quizzes submitted when time runs out, courses complete when every lesson is done and can be retaken) and WooCommerce (no remote logging, marketplace suggestions or Order attribution tracking).
* Fix: Plugin presets: Reset to defaults now removes settings the plugin had not stored before, instead of leaving them empty.
* New, on by default: Quiet Freemius prompts. Plugins that use Freemius, such as WP Sheet Editor and Git Updater, stop asking you to opt in, offering upgrades, add-ons and trials, opening their opt-in page when activated and asking why you deactivate them. The Plugins screen also drops the hidden dialogs they add. Licences, accounts and support stay, and nothing is changed in those plugins. Switch it off to get the prompts back.
* New: Load plugins only where needed can also skip admin tools you choose when people view the site, so pages that are not cached open faster. SEO Pro Stack checks what each plugin adds to the site and says so next to it. Plugins that handle logins or that other plugins need always load, and logins, forms and links with extra arguments still load every plugin. If a page fails, the site loads every plugin again and the settings say where.
* Change: with Load plugins only where needed, SEO Pro Stack’s own settings load only the plugins they need, so each tab opens faster (on a test site with 85 plugins, about 1.3 seconds instead of 2 to 4).
* Fix: Tidy the dashboard keeps offering widgets of plugins that the Dashboard does not load.
* Fix: with Load plugins only where needed, the Dashboard shows plugins’ boxes again. It loads only the plugins whose boxes show there; hide a box with Hide dashboard widgets or Tidy the dashboard and its plugin is skipped there too.
* Change: with Load plugins only where needed, Appearance › Menus loads only the plugins that change menus or own what can be added to them, with the same boxes, locations and fields.
* Change: with Load plugins only where needed, Appearance › Editor loads only the plugins that add blocks, editor features, templates or styles.
* Fix: Organise the admin menu opens the right menu on Kadence Blocks’ Forms, Navigations and Headers, and on other plugins’ lists shown in their own menu.
* Fix: with Load plugins only where needed, a screen that failed while Query Monitor was active kept failing (such as Admin Columns’ settings). It now loads every plugin after the first failure.
* Fix: Hide admin notices keeps empty boxes that plugins fill later hidden, so admin screens no longer move down as they load.
* Fix: Tidy the dashboard no longer leaves a large empty space in a column without boxes, which pushed the boxes below down as the Dashboard loaded.

* Change: Load plugins only where needed also speeds up the Profile, Edit user and Add user screens, Tools, Media › Add New and the About screens. Plugins that show or save profile fields still load on the profile screens. The Plugins menu in the admin bar says why a screen loads every plugin.
* New, off by default: Readable list columns (Admin tab) keeps the title column of post, page, user and other lists wide enough to read when plugins add columns of their own, and stops other columns being squeezed out. The other columns get narrower instead. Lists are fitted before they show, so they never flash up squeezed.
* Fix: with Load plugins only where needed, WP Sheet Editor’s pages opened blank. Pages that Freemius takes over now load the plugin they belong to.
* Fix: Meow Apps’ logo showed full size next to a cog on some screens. It now shows as the menu icon.
* Change: Organise the admin menu leaves out upgrade links (Upgrade, Go Pro, Get Pro and the like), entries without a name, setup pages WordPress never shows (WooCommerce’s Setup Wizard), Freesoul Deactivate Plugins’ second menu, Freemius opt-in prompts and MasterStudy’s “Unlock this addon” pages. Their pages still open; choose a place for an entry to show it again. Pages with the same name in Developers › Settings show the menu they came from.
* New, off by default: Faster editor with Kadence Blocks (Speed tab) loads Kadence’s design library when you open it, instead of in every editor page. On a test site the new post screen went from about 24 MB to under 9 MB.
* Fix: with Load plugins only where needed, Organise the admin menu puts the entries of plugins a screen skips in the same places as on other screens.
* Fix: the admin menu keeps its order when a plugin’s script moves entries (MasterStudy LMS moved WooCommerce Analytics above Posts).
* Change: Organise the admin menu knows more plugins: MasterStudy LMS (Content), Lasso Lite and Revive.so (SEO), and GOTMLS, FluentSnippets, Easy Code Manager, Hreflang Manager Lite, Plugin Check, Git Updater and Disable WordPress Updates (Developers).
* Change: Tidy the dashboard also hides MasterStudy’s announcements and news and WP Sheet Editor’s usage stats.
* Fix: Hide admin notices moves WooCommerce’s lasting notices and plain message boxes such as MainWP Child’s behind the bell.
* New, off by default: Plugin presets (Plugins tab) applies SEO Pro Stack’s chosen settings to other plugins from the Plugins screen, resets them to the plugin’s defaults or undoes the last change, each after a confirmation, also as bulk actions. Licence keys, API keys and passwords are never stored or changed. First presets: Antispam Bee and Simple CAPTCHA with Cloudflare Turnstile. New `wp seoprostack presets` command (list, diff, apply, reset, undo, export).

= 0.5.0 =
* New: Free Plugins installs, activates, deactivates and uninstalls plugins without leaving the page. Activate becomes Deactivate, and a deactivated plugin offers Uninstall. It uses the full screen width, and its categories stay on one line.
* New: the All view in Free Plugins lists every recommended plugin by category, with checkboxes and bulk actions (Install and activate, Install, Activate, Deactivate, Uninstall) for setting up new sites quickly.
* New, off by default: Tidy the dashboard (Admin tab) lays out the Dashboard the same way on every site, hides the Welcome panel, news and plugin promotions, and shows Site Health to developers only and statistics to people who can publish.
* New: copies installed from GitHub releases get updates from GitHub through the free Git Updater plugin. A notice, and Free Plugins, offer to install and activate it in one click.
* New, off by default, GitHub copies only: Early updates from GitHub (Maintenance tab) keeps the site on GitHub releases, which come out before WordPress.org.
* New: a star in the admin bar, next to your name, opens SEO Pro Stack’s settings, for people who can change them.
* New, off by default: Organise the admin menu (Admin tab) groups the menu into sections, with Administrators and Developers menus and Users under Admin, the same way on every site, with places chosen from a list, sections that can fold and role previews, with client safeguards for people who are not developers (on by default). Replaces Admin Menu Editor.
* New, off by default: More menu in the admin bar (Admin tab) puts the items plugins and themes add to the left of the admin bar in one … menu, last on that side, so the bar no longer wraps over the page. It opens on hover, like the other admin bar menus. Choose plugins whose items stay on the bar. On phones the menu also reaches plugin items WordPress hides there.
* New, on by default: Hide admin bar items (Admin tab) removes WordPress items you do not use from the admin bar, for everyone. Comments and + New are ticked by default. Switch it off to keep every item.
* New, on by default: No fade between admin screens (Admin tab) stops the fade WordPress 7.0 added between wp-admin screens, which can flash, so screens change straight away. Switch it off to keep the fade.
* New: Load pages before the click can also preload admin screens (off by default). Only the page is downloaded, and links that change something are skipped.
* Change: Hide the admin bar and Block dashboard access no longer offer Administrator, since administrators are never affected.
* Change: roles that plugins add, such as WooCommerce’s Customer, are ticked in Hide the admin bar and Block dashboard access unless they can write posts. With WooCommerce, blocked users go to My Account unless you chose another page.
* Change: on new sites, SVG uploads also allows authors, contributors and shop managers, and watermarks are 10% of the picture. Existing settings are kept.
* Change: Resize large uploads no longer imports Imsanity’s default limit (1920), so 2560 is used.
* Change: the Plugins menu always sits next to the account menu in the admin bar, with the notices bell to its left and other plugins’ items further left.
* Change: the notices bell is on every admin screen, with a white dot instead of a count while there are notices, so nothing on the admin bar moves as the page loads. With no notices, its panel says so.
* New: with Load plugins only where needed, the notices of plugins a screen skips are kept and shown behind the bell there, so they can be read and dismissed from any screen.
* Fix: only notices are kept from skipped plugins, so buttons a plugin prints above its own list (such as Kadence Blocks’ Export All and Import) no longer appear on the Dashboard and other screens.
* Change: the notices panel opens when you point at the bell and closes when you move away, like the other admin bar menus.
* Change: the notices bell, the Plugins icon and the More menu no longer show a tooltip over their open menus. Screen readers still announce them.
* New: with Load plugins only where needed, the admin bar can also check every screen again. Both reload options ask first and explain what will happen.
* New, off by default: Hosting needs (Plugins tab) shows whether your hosting fits the site, with the OPcache and PHP memory settings to ask your host for, the memory each PHP worker needs, and hosting to buy for low, medium and high traffic and for the traffic measured now. Shown below the plugin list, as two Site Health tests and on the Site Health Info tab.
* New: Short links starts you with /googlereview/, /facebookreview/ and /trustpilotreview/ links in a Review Requests category. Point them at your own review pages, then use them in email signatures and review requests.

= 0.4.0 =
* Settings are grouped into Admin, Content, Media, Links and Speed tabs. Old links still open the right tab.
* New: search features by name, description or the plugin they replace, and change them from the results.
* New Plugins tab, all off by default: a Plugins menu in the admin bar (replaces Plugin Toggle), a Size column on the Plugins screen with totals for installed and active plugins, and cleanup of leftover entries for deleted plugins.
* Free Plugins no longer lists Plugin Toggle or String Locator.
* Free Plugins no longer lists EditorsKit, last updated in May 2024. WordPress and Kadence Blocks cover its features.
* New, off by default: Hide admin notices moves plugin and theme notices behind a bell in the admin bar, with a count (replaces Hide Admin Notices). “Show example notices” adds one of each kind to try it.
* New, off by default: Paste into the Media Library uploads pasted screenshots, pictures and files (replaces The Paste and imports its settings).
* New, off by default: Avatars without Gravatar serves avatars from your own site, with profile picture uploads (replaces Avatar Privacy and copies its profile pictures).
* New, off by default: SVG uploads (replaces Safe SVG), Resize large uploads (replaces Imsanity) and Replace media files (replaces Enable Media Replace), each importing that plugin’s settings.
* New, off by default: WebP and AVIF images makes smaller copies of every picture and sends them to browsers that support them (replaces CompressX, imports its settings and reuses the copies it made).
* New, off by default: Watermark pictures adds the site icon, logo or a chosen picture to uploaded pictures and keeps unmarked originals so watermarks can be removed (replaces Easy Watermark and imports its image watermark).
* New, off by default: Short addresses for custom post types serves items at /item-name/ instead of /type/item-name/ and redirects the old addresses (replaces Remove CPT base and imports its post types).
* New, off by default: Short links (Links tab) makes short addresses that redirect elsewhere, with categories and click counts (replaces Pretty Links: imports its links, click counts and categories, and its defaults for new links).
* New, off by default: Load plugins only where needed (Plugins tab) makes wp-admin faster on sites with many plugins by loading ticked plugins only on the screens that need them. The Plugins menu in the admin bar shows how many loaded and can reload a screen with every plugin to check it again.
* New, off by default: Website screenshots adds a Screenshot block and the `[browser-shot]` shortcode, with pictures saved to the Media Library and served from your site (replaces Browser Shots, whose shortcode and blocks keep working).
* New, off by default: Spectra block replacements adds a Term list block and keeps pages built with Spectra working after it is deactivated, with a Convert button for its blocks in the editor (replaces Spectra).
* Change: while a plugin that a feature replaces is active, the feature waits, so the two no longer run side by side (such as two Plugins menus, or analytics loaded twice). The card says so, with a deactivate link.
* Change: Hide admin notices also moves inline notices printed above the page, notices inside other plugins’ wrappers, notices added by scripts while the page loads, and WooCommerce’s script-drawn notices.
* Fix: `%date%` and `%day%` in Copy linked images’ file name and alt text patterns are no longer lost when saved.

= 0.3.1 =
* Free plugin lists show changes straight away instead of after the 12-hour cache expires.
* The Flying Press card no longer recommends Flying Analytics, Flying Pages or Flying Scripts, which the Speed tab replaces.

= 0.3.0 =
* Renamed from WP Allstars to SEO Pro Stack. Settings are migrated automatically.
* New: magic login links, publishing queue and iFrame block (all off by default).
* New, replacing separate plugins and importing their settings: admin bar and dashboard access, widget control, notification emails, duplicate posts, staged new versions, shareable preview links, sticky posts for any post type, select all across pages, 410 Gone addresses, page preloading, delayed scripts and delayed Google Analytics.
* New Speed tab.
* Kadence links point to the current Kadence pages at Liquid Web.
* Setting cards keep their rounded corners and “on” marker when hovered.
* New admin screen with grouped tabs, instant-save setting cards, accessible switches and expandable options.
* Settings stored in one option with automatic migration from earlier versions.
* Modern admin colours switches live and updates your profile preference.
* Auto upload images rewritten and renamed “Copy linked images to Media Library”: safer downloads, de-duplication, domain exclusions, resizing, name and alt patterns.
* Free plugin cards use core install and activate buttons; theme installs in place.
* Filterable Pro Plugins, Hosting and Tools directories.
* Uninstall cleanup, including multisite.
